1	/**
2	 * VideoLightbox
3	 *
4	 * Specialized lightbox for video media. Handles all video-specific functionality:
5	 * - Video trimming
6	 * - Video replace mode
7	 * - Video regenerate mode
8	 * - Video enhance mode
9	 * - Segment slot mode (timeline navigation)
10	 *
11	 * Uses useSharedLightboxState for shared functionality (variants, navigation, etc.)
12	 *
13	 * This is part of the split architecture where MediaLightbox dispatches to
14	 * ImageLightbox or VideoLightbox based on media type.
15	 */
16	
17	import React from 'react';
18	import type { VideoLightboxProps, VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia } from './types';
19	import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
20	import type { OverlayViewportConstraints } from '@/shared/lib/layout/overlayViewportConstraints';
21	
22	import { LightboxProviders } from './components/LightboxProviders';
23	import { LightboxLayout } from './components/layouts/LightboxLayout';
24	import { SegmentSlotFormView } from './components/SegmentSlotFormView';
25	
26	import {
27	  useVideoLightboxMode,
28	  useVideoLightboxEnvironment,
29	} from './hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxEnvironment';
30	import { useVideoLightboxRenderModel } from './hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel';
31	import {
32	  useVideoLightboxSharedState,
33	  useVideoLightboxEditing,
34	} from './hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxController';
35	
36	import { LightboxShell } from './components/LightboxShell';
37	import { VideoEditProvider } from './contexts/VideoEditContext';
38	
39	export type {
40	  VideoLightboxSharedStateModel,
41	  VideoLightboxEditModel,
42	} from './hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxController';
43	
44	const VideoLightboxMediaContent: React.FC<VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia> = (props) => {
45	  const modeModel = useVideoLightboxMode(props);
46	  const env = useVideoLightboxEnvironment(props, modeModel);
47	  const sharedState = useVideoLightboxSharedState(props, modeModel, env);
48	  const editModel = useVideoLightboxEditing(props, modeModel, env, sharedState);
49	  const renderModel = useVideoLightboxRenderModel(props, modeModel, env, sharedState, editModel);
50	  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
51	    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
52	    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
53	    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
54	    isTabletOrLarger: sharedState.layout.isTabletOrLarger,
55	    needsFullscreenLayout: renderModel.needsFullscreenLayout,
56	    needsTasksPaneOffset: renderModel.needsTasksPaneOffset,
57	  };
58	
59	  return (
60	    <LightboxProviders stateValue={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
61	      <VideoEditProvider value={editModel.videoEditValue}>
62	        <LightboxShell
63	          onClose={props.onClose}
64	          hasCanvasOverlay={false}
65	          isRepositionMode={false}
66	          isMobile={env.isMobile}
67	          overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
68	          contentRef={env.contentRef}
69	          accessibilityTitle={renderModel.accessibilityTitle}
70	          accessibilityDescription={renderModel.accessibilityDescription}
71	        >
72	          {modeModel.isFormOnlyMode && props.segmentSlotMode ? (
73	            <SegmentSlotFormView
74	              segmentSlotMode={props.segmentSlotMode}
75	              onClose={props.onClose}
76	              onNavPrev={modeModel.handleSlotNavPrev}
77	              onNavNext={modeModel.handleSlotNavNext}
78	              hasPrevious={modeModel.hasPrevious}
79	              hasNext={modeModel.hasNext}
80	              readOnly={props.readOnly}
81	            />
82	          ) : (
83	            <LightboxLayout {...renderModel.layoutProps} controlsPanelContent={renderModel.controlsPanelContent} />
84	          )}
85	        </LightboxShell>
86	      </VideoEditProvider>
87	    </LightboxProviders>
88	  );
89	};
90	
91	const VideoLightboxFormOnlyContent: React.FC<VideoLightboxProps> = (props) => {
92	  const modeModel = useVideoLightboxMode(props);
93	  const env = useVideoLightboxEnvironment(props, modeModel);
94	
95	  if (!props.segmentSlotMode) {
96	    return null;
97	  }
98	
99	  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
100	    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
101	    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
102	    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
103	    isTabletOrLarger: false,
104	    needsFullscreenLayout: true,
105	    needsTasksPaneOffset: false,
106	  };
107	
108	  return (
109	    <LightboxShell
110	      onClose={props.onClose}
111	      hasCanvasOverlay={false}
112	      isRepositionMode={false}
113	      isMobile={env.isMobile}
114	      overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
115	      contentRef={env.contentRef}
116	      accessibilityTitle={`Segment ${(props.segmentSlotMode.currentIndex ?? 0) + 1} Settings`}
117	      accessibilityDescription="Configure and generate this video segment. Use Tab or arrow keys to navigate between segments."
118	    >
119	      <SegmentSlotFormView
120	        segmentSlotMode={props.segmentSlotMode}
121	        onClose={props.onClose}
122	        onNavPrev={modeModel.handleSlotNavPrev}
123	        onNavNext={modeModel.handleSlotNavNext}
124	        hasPrevious={modeModel.hasPrevious}
125	        hasNext={modeModel.hasNext}
126	        readOnly={props.readOnly}
127	      />
128	    </LightboxShell>
129	  );
130	};
131	
132	export const VideoLightbox: React.FC<VideoLightboxProps> = (props) => {
133	  const isSegmentSlotMode = !!props.segmentSlotMode;
134	  const hasSegmentVideo = isSegmentSlotMode && !!props.segmentSlotMode?.segmentVideo;
135	  const isFormOnlyMode = isSegmentSlotMode && !hasSegmentVideo;
136	
137	  if (isFormOnlyMode) {
138	    return <VideoLightboxFormOnlyContent {...props} />;
139	  }
140	
141	  if (!props.media) {
142	    normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('No media prop provided and not in form-only mode'), {
143	      context: 'VideoLightbox',
144	      showToast: false,
145	    });
146	    return null;
147	  }
148	
149	  const media = props.parentGenerationIdOverride
150	    ? {
151	        ...props.media,
152	        parent_generation_id: props.parentGenerationIdOverride,
153	      }
154	    : props.media;
155	
156	  return <VideoLightboxMediaContent {...props} media={media} />;
157	};
158

387	  const model = coerceSelectedModel(settings.selectedModel);
388	  const frames = settings.batchVideoFrames || 61;
389	  const prompt = settings.prompt || '';
390	  const truncatedPrompt = prompt.length > 60 ? prompt.slice(0, 57) + '...' : prompt;
391	
392	  return (
393	    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-400">
394	      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-300">{model}</span>

422	export function VideoGenerationModalAccordionContent({
423	  defaultTopOpen = false,
424	  defaultFinalVideoOpen = false,
425	  defaultBottomOpen = true,
426	  shotId,
427	  projectId,
428	  positionedImages,
429	  shotGenerations,
430	  effectiveAspectRatio,

432	}: VideoGenerationModalAccordionContentProps) {

434	  const hasFinalVideo = segmentOutputs.parentGenerations.some((p) => Boolean(p.location));
435	  const imageHandlers = useModalImageHandlers(shotId, projectId, shotGenerations, formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61);
436	
437	  return (
438	    <div className="space-y-4">

442	          defaultOpen={defaultFinalVideoOpen}
443	          summary={<FinalVideoSummary />}
444	        >
445	          <FinalVideoSection
446	            shotId={shotId}
447	            projectId={projectId}
448	            projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
449	            onDelete={imageHandlers.onDeleteFinalVideo}
450	          />
451	        </ModalAccordionSection>
452	      )}
453	
454	      <ModalAccordionSection
455	        title="Shot Images"
456	        defaultOpen={defaultTopOpen}
457	        summary={<ShotImagesSummary images={positionedImages} />}
458	      >
459	        <ModalShotImagesEditor
460	          shotId={shotId}
461	          projectId={projectId}
462	          images={shotGenerations}
463	          batchVideoFrames={formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61}
464	          projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
465	        />
466	      </ModalAccordionSection>
467	
468	      <ModalAccordionSection
469	        title="Generation Settings"
470	        defaultOpen={defaultBottomOpen}
471	        summary={<GenerationSettingsSummary settings={formProps.settings} />}
472	      >
473	        <VideoGenerationModalFormContent {...formProps} />
474	      </ModalAccordionSection>
475	    </div>
476	  );

271	        {/* Segment Settings Form - hidden in readOnly mode */}
272	        <div className="p-4">
273	          {readOnly ? (
274	            <div className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-8">
275	              <p className="text-sm">Segment {segmentSlotMode.currentIndex + 1}</p>
276	              <p className="text-xs mt-1">No video generated yet</p>
277	            </div>
278	          ) : (
279	            <>
280	              <SegmentSettingsForm
281	                {...formProps}
282	                modelName={selectedModelName}
283	                structureVideoDefaults={effectiveStructureVideoDefaults ?? undefined}
284	                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
285	                onFrameCountChange={handleFrameCountChange}
286	              />
287	
288	              {/* Show warning if missing context */}
289	              {!segmentSlotMode.parentGenerationId && !segmentSlotMode.shotId && (
290	                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center mt-2">
291	                  Cannot generate: Missing shot context. Please save your shot first.
292	                </p>
293	              )}
294	            </>
295	          )}
296	        </div>
297	      </div>
298	      </div>
299	      </div>
300	    </div>
301	  );
302	};
303

129	  // Model change handler (shared between PromptSection toggle and AdvancedSettingsSection)
130	  const handleModelChange = useModelChange({ onChange, settings, shotDefaults });
131	
132	  // Save-field-as-default (shared with AdvancedSettingsSection)
133	  const { savingField, handleSaveFieldAsDefault } = useSaveFieldAsDefault({
134	    onSaveFieldAsDefault,
135	    onChange,
136	  });
137	
138	  // Structure video upload hook
139	  const videoUpload = useStructureVideoUpload({
140	    structureVideoFrameRange,
141	    settings,
142	    structureVideoDefaults,
143	    onAddSegmentStructureVideo,
144	  });
145	
146	  // Prompt field state
147	  const promptField = usePromptFieldState({
148	    settingsPrompt: settings.prompt,
149	    enhancedPrompt,
150	    basePromptForEnhancement,
151	    defaultPrompt: shotDefaults?.prompt,
152	    onSettingsChange: (value) => onChange({ prompt: value }),
153	    onClearEnhancedPrompt,
154	  });
155	
156	  useEffect(() => {
157	    setStartImageUrlOverride(null);
158	  }, [startImageUrl]);
159	
160	  useEffect(() => {
161	    setEndImageUrlOverride(null);
162	  }, [endImageUrl]);
163	
164	  // ==========================================================================
165	  // HANDLERS
166	  // ==========================================================================
167	
168	  const handleSubmit = useCallback(async () => {
169	    setSubmitSuccess(false);
170	    try {
171	      await onSubmit();
172	      setSubmitSuccess(true);
173	      setTimeout(() => setSubmitSuccess(false), 1500);
174	    } catch (error) {
175	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'SegmentSettingsForm', showToast: false });
176	    }
177	  }, [onSubmit]);
178	
179	  const handleFrameCountChange = useCallback((value: number) => {
180	    const quantized = quantizeFrameCount(value, 9, frameStep);
181	    onChange({ numFrames: quantized });
182	    onFrameCountChange?.(quantized);
183	  }, [frameStep, onChange, onFrameCountChange]);
184	
185	  const handleSaveAsShotDefaults = useCallback(async () => {
186	    if (!onSaveAsShotDefaults) return;
187	    setIsSavingDefaults(true);
188	    setSaveDefaultsSuccess(false);
189	    try {
190	      const success = await onSaveAsShotDefaults();
191	      if (success) {
192	        setSaveDefaultsSuccess(true);
193	        setTimeout(() => setSaveDefaultsSuccess(false), 2000);
194	      }
195	    } finally {
196	      setIsSavingDefaults(false);
197	    }
198	  }, [onSaveAsShotDefaults]);
199	
200	  // Drag and drop handlers for video upload
201	  const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
202	    e.preventDefault();
203	    e.stopPropagation();
204	  }, []);
205	
206	  const handleDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
207	    e.preventDefault();
208	    e.stopPropagation();
209	    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')) {
210	      setIsDraggingVideo(true);
211	    }
212	  }, []);
213	
214	  const handleDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
215	    e.preventDefault();
216	    e.stopPropagation();
217	    if (e.currentTarget === e.target || !e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) {
218	      setIsDraggingVideo(false);
219	    }
220	  }, []);
221	
222	  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
223	    e.preventDefault();
224	    e.stopPropagation();
225	    setIsDraggingVideo(false);
226	
227	    const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
228	    if (files.length > 0) {
229	      const file = files[0];
230	      if (file.type.startsWith('video/')) {
231	        videoUpload.handleFileSelect({ target: { files } } as unknown as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>);
232	      }
233	    }
234	  }, [videoUpload]);
235	
236	  // ==========================================================================
237	  // RENDER
238	  // ==========================================================================
239	
240	  return (
241	    <div className="space-y-4">
242	      {/* Header */}
243	      {showHeader && (
244	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
245	          <h3 className="text-lg font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
246	            <RotateCcw className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
247	            {headerTitle}
248	          </h3>
249	        </div>
250	      )}
251	
252	      {/* Input Images with Frames Slider */}
253	      {(displayedStartImageUrl || displayedEndImageUrl) && (
254	        <div className="@container">
255	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 @[280px]:grid-cols-3">
256	            {/* Start Image */}
257	            <div className="relative aspect-video">
258	              {displayedStartImageUrl && (
259	                <button
260	                  type="button"
261	                  onClick={() => startImageShotGenerationId && onNavigateToImage?.(startImageShotGenerationId)}
262	                  disabled={!onNavigateToImage || !startImageShotGenerationId}
263	                  className="w-full h-full relative bg-muted/30 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/50 transition-all hover:ring-2 hover:ring-primary/50 hover:scale-[1.02] disabled:hover:ring-0 disabled:hover:scale-100 disabled:cursor-default"
264	                  title={onNavigateToImage && startImageShotGenerationId ? "View start image" : undefined}
265	                >
266	                  {startImageGenerationId && startImageUrl && (
267	                    <ImageVariantPicker
268	                      generationId={startImageGenerationId}
269	                      currentImageUrl={startImageUrl}
270	                      onImageUrlChange={setStartImageUrlOverride}
271	                    />
272	                  )}
273	                  <img
274	                    src={displayedStartImageUrl}
275	                    alt="Start frame"
276	                    className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
277	                  />
278	                  <span className="absolute bottom-0.5 left-0.5 text-[10px] bg-black/60 text-white px-1 rounded">Start</span>
279	                </button>
280	              )}
281	            </div>
282	
283	            {/* Frames Slider */}
284	            <div className="order-last col-span-2 @[280px]:order-none @[280px]:col-span-1 flex items-center gap-2">
285	              <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center gap-y-1">
286	                <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
287	                  <Label className="text-xs font-medium">Frames</Label>
288	                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
289	                    {settings.numFrames} ({framesToSeconds(settings.numFrames)})
290	                  </span>
291	                </div>
292	                <Slider
293	                  value={quantizeFrameCount(settings.numFrames, 9, frameStep)}
294	                  onValueChange={(value) => {
295	                    const nextValue = Array.isArray(value)
296	                      ? (value[0] ?? quantizeFrameCount(settings.numFrames, 9, frameStep))
297	                      : value;
298	                    handleFrameCountChange(nextValue);
299	                  }}
300	                  min={9}
301	                  max={resolvedMaxFrames}
302	                  step={frameStep}
303	                  className="w-full"
304	                />
305	              </div>
306	            </div>
307	
308	            {/* End Image */}
309	            <div className="relative aspect-video">
310	              {displayedEndImageUrl && (
311	                <button
312	                  type="button"
313	                  onClick={() => endImageShotGenerationId && onNavigateToImage?.(endImageShotGenerationId)}
314	                  disabled={!onNavigateToImage || !endImageShotGenerationId}
315	                  className="w-full h-full relative bg-muted/30 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/50 transition-all hover:ring-2 hover:ring-primary/50 hover:scale-[1.02] disabled:hover:ring-0 disabled:hover:scale-100 disabled:cursor-default"
316	                  title={onNavigateToImage && endImageShotGenerationId ? "View end image" : undefined}
317	                >
318	                  {endImageGenerationId && endImageUrl && (
319	                    <ImageVariantPicker
320	                      generationId={endImageGenerationId}
321	                      currentImageUrl={endImageUrl}
322	                      onImageUrlChange={setEndImageUrlOverride}
323	                    />
324	                  )}
325	                  <img
326	                    src={displayedEndImageUrl}
327	                    alt="End frame"
328	                    className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
329	                  />
330	                  <span className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 text-[10px] bg-black/60 text-white px-1 rounded">End</span>
331	                </button>
332	              )}
333	            </div>
334	          </div>
335	        </div>
336	      )}
337	
338	      {/* Prompt */}
339	      <PromptSection
340	        promptField={promptField}
341	        isRegeneration={isRegeneration}
342	        settings={settings}
343	        onChange={onChange}
344	        selectedModel={effectiveSelectedModel}
345	        onSelectedModelChange={handleModelChange}
346	        basePromptForEnhancement={basePromptForEnhancement}
347	        enhancePromptEnabled={enhancePromptEnabled}
348	        onEnhancePromptChange={onEnhancePromptChange}
349	        onSaveFieldAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault}
350	        handleSaveFieldAsDefault={handleSaveFieldAsDefault}
351	        savingField={savingField}
352	        smoothContinuations={canToggleSmoothContinuations ? {
353	          enabled: effectiveSmoothContinuations,
354	          onChange: (checked: boolean) => onChange({ smoothContinuations: checked }),
355	          maxOutputFrames: continuationPolicy.continuation.maxOutputFrames,
356	        } : undefined}
357	      />
358	
359	      {/* Advanced Settings */}
360	      <AdvancedSettingsSection
361	        settings={settings}
362	        onChange={onChange}
363	        modelName={modelName}
364	        queryKeyPrefix={queryKeyPrefix}
365	        edgeExtendAmount={edgeExtendAmount}
366	        defaults={{
367	          shotDefaults,
368	          hasOverride,
369	          onSaveFieldAsDefault,
370	        }}
371	        structureVideo={{
372	          type: structureVideoType,
373	          url: structureVideoUrl,
374	          frameRange: structureVideoFrameRange,
375	          defaults: structureVideoDefaults,
376	          isTimelineMode,
377	          onAddSegmentStructureVideo,
378	          onRemoveSegmentStructureVideo,
379	        }}
380	        videoInteraction={{
381	          videoUpload,
382	          isDraggingVideo,
383	          onDragOver: handleDragOver,
384	          onDragEnter: handleDragEnter,
385	          onDragLeave: handleDragLeave,
386	          onDrop: handleDrop,
387	        }}
388	      />
389	
390	      {/* Submit Button */}
391	      <Button
392	        size="sm"
393	        onClick={handleSubmit}
394	        disabled={isSubmitting || !startImageUrl}
395	        className="w-full gap-2"
396	        variant={submitSuccess ? "outline" : "default"}
397	      >
398	        {isSubmitting ? (
399	          <>
400	            <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
401	            <span>Generating...</span>
402	          </>
403	        ) : submitSuccess ? (
404	          <>
405	            <span className="text-green-600">&#10003;</span>
406	            <span>Task Created</span>
407	          </>
408	        ) : (
409	          <span>{buttonLabel || (isRegeneration ? 'Regenerate Segment' : 'Generate Segment')}</span>
410	        )}
411	      </Button>
412	
413	      {/* Restore Defaults / Save as Defaults Buttons */}
414	      {(onRestoreDefaults || onSaveAsShotDefaults) && (
415	        <div className="flex gap-2">
416	          {onRestoreDefaults && (
417	            <Button
418	              size="sm"
419	              variant="ghost"
420	              onClick={onRestoreDefaults}
421	              disabled={isSubmitting || isSavingDefaults}
422	              className="flex-1 h-7 text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground gap-1"
423	            >
424	              <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
425	              Restore Defaults
426	            </Button>
427	          )}
428	          {onSaveAsShotDefaults && (
429	            <Button
430	              size="sm"
431	              variant="ghost"
432	              onClick={handleSaveAsShotDefaults}
433	              disabled={isSubmitting || isSavingDefaults}
434	              className="flex-1 h-7 text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground gap-1"
435	            >
436	              {isSavingDefaults ? (
437	                <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
438	              ) : saveDefaultsSuccess ? (
439	                <span className="text-green-600">&#10003;</span>
440	              ) : (
441	                <Save className="w-3 h-3" />
442	              )}
443	              {saveDefaultsSuccess ? 'Saved!' : 'Set as Shot Defaults'}
444	            </Button>
445	          )}
446	        </div>
447	      )}
448	    </div>
449	  );
450	};
451

Based on my analysis, I now have enough information to provide a comprehensive comparison. Let me compile the findings:

## Comparison of Two Video Generation UI Surfaces

### (a) File Paths

1. **VideoLightbox (Media Lightbox):** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/VideoLightbox.tsx`
   - Renders: `SegmentSlotFormView` → `SegmentSettingsForm` (in `/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/`)

2. **VideoGenerationModal (Travel Tool):** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGenerationModal.tsx`
   - Renders: `VideoGenerationModalAccordionContent` → `BatchSettingsForm` + `MotionControl` (in `/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/`)

---

### (b) Concrete Field Lists

**SegmentSettingsForm (Lightbox - Segment/Timeline context):**
- Prompt field (with enhancement option)
- Negative prompt
- Start/end image previews with frame slider
- Advanced section (collapsible, hidden by default):
  - Before/after prompt text
  - Motion mode toggle (basic/advanced)
  - LoRA selector
  - Structure video upload (drag-drop)
  - Phase config (for advanced mode)
  - Random seed toggle

**BatchSettingsForm + MotionControl (Modal - Batch context):**
- Main prompt + negative prompt (side-by-side)
- Additional prompt settings (collapsible): before/after text
- Model toggle (explicit, always visible)
- Enhance Prompts toggle
- HD Resolution toggle (model-dependent)
- Smooth Continuations toggle (model-dependent)
- Dimension controls (project/firstImage/custom)
- Custom width/height fields
- Frame count + steps sliders
- Amount of motion slider
- Accelerated/turbo mode toggles
- Random seed toggle
- MotionControl section (right column):
  - Motion mode tabs (basic/advanced)
  - LoRA selection
  - Phase presets
  - Advanced settings (phase config, random seed, motion presets)

---

### (c) Key Differences & Likely Reason

**Fields ONLY in SegmentSettingsForm:**
- Start/end image thumbnails with navigation
- Structure video upload (drag-drop)

**Fields ONLY in BatchSettingsForm/MotionControl:**
- Dimension controls (project/firstImage/custom width/height)
- Frame steps slider
- Amount of motion slider
- Accelerated mode
- Turbo mode
- Smooth continuations
- HD Resolution toggle

**Why the divergence:**

1. **Different use cases** — Segment form is for **per-segment timeline editing** (narrowly scoped, regenerating one shot segment); Modal is for **batch video generation** (all segments at once with global settings).

2. **Timeline vs Batch execution** — SegmentSettingsForm passes `isTimelineMode=true` and manages structure video per-segment. BatchSettingsForm always operates in batch mode (`videoControlMode="batch"`), making batch-specific controls like turbo/accelerated/dimension-source relevant.

3. **Visibility & complexity** — Segment form keeps most advanced options hidden (collapsed Advanced section); Modal exposes motion/phase controls in a dedicated MotionControl panel for discoverable, full control.

4. **Intentional product design** — The segment flow guides users toward image-first workflows; the batch modal enables power-user control over global motion/structure. No feature flags or version markers indicate this divergence was accidental.

209	                <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-full md:w-[200px]">
210	                  <Select value={treatment} onValueChange={onTreatmentChange} disabled={readOnly}>
211	                    <SelectTrigger variant="retro" size="sm" className="h-9 w-full">
212	                      <SelectValue>
213	                        {treatment === 'adjust'
214	                          ? (controller.totalVideoFrames > controller.timelineFrames
215	                            ? 'Compress'
216	                            : controller.totalVideoFrames < controller.timelineFrames
217	                              ? 'Stretch'
218	                              : 'Match') + ' to timeline'
219	                          : 'Use video as is'}
220	                      </SelectValue>
221	                    </SelectTrigger>
222	                    <SelectContent variant="retro">
223	                      <SelectItem variant="retro" value="adjust">
224	                        {controller.totalVideoFrames > controller.timelineFrames
225	                          ? 'Compress'
226	                          : controller.totalVideoFrames < controller.timelineFrames
227	                            ? 'Stretch'
228	                            : 'Match'} to timeline
229	                      </SelectItem>
230	                      <SelectItem variant="retro" value="clip">
231	                        Use video as is
232	                      </SelectItem>
233	                    </SelectContent>
234	                  </Select>
235	                </div>
236	                <div className="flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">
237	                  {treatment === 'adjust' ? controller.adjustModeDescription : controller.clipModeDescription}
238	                </div>
239	              </div>
240	            </div>
241	
242	            <div className="mt-auto pt-2 hidden md:block">
243	              {!readOnly && (
244	                <Button
245	                  variant="outline"
246	                  size="sm"
247	                  className="w-full text-destructive hover:text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10"
248	                  onClick={controller.handleRemoveVideo}
249	                >
250	                  <X className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
251	                  Remove Video
252	                </Button>
253	              )}
254	            </div>
255	          </div>
256	        </div>
257	      </div>
258	    </div>
259	  );
260	};

305	  const [generationMode, setGenerationMode] = useState<'batch' | 'timeline'>('batch');
306	  const handlers = useModalImageHandlers(shotId, projectId, images, batchVideoFrames);
307	
308	  const editorProps: ShotImagesEditorProps = useMemo(() => ({
309	    displayOptions: {
310	      isModeReady: true,
311	      isMobile: false,
312	      generationMode,
313	      onGenerationModeChange: setGenerationMode as (mode: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair') => void,
314	      columns: 4 as const,
315	      skeleton: <ImageManagerSkeleton />,
316	      projectAspectRatio,
317	    },
318	    imageState: {
319	      selectedShotId: shotId,
320	      projectId,
321	      preloadedImages: images,
322	      batchVideoFrames,
323	      pendingPositions: emptyMap,
324	      unpositionedGenerationsCount: 0,
325	      fileInputKey: 0,
326	      isUploadingImage: handlers.isUploadingImage,
327	      uploadProgress: handlers.uploadProgress,
328	    },
329	    editActions: {
330	      onImageReorder: handlers.onImageReorder,
331	      onFramePositionsChange: noop,
332	      onFileDrop: handlers.onFileDrop,
333	      onGenerationDrop: handlers.onGenerationDrop,
334	      onBatchFileDrop: handlers.onBatchFileDrop,
335	      onBatchGenerationDrop: handlers.onBatchGenerationDrop,
336	      onPendingPositionApplied: noop,
337	      onImageDelete: handlers.onImageDelete,
338	      onBatchImageDelete: handlers.onBatchImageDelete,
339	      onImageDuplicate: handlers.onImageDuplicate,
340	      onOpenUnpositionedPane: noop,
341	      onImageUpload: handlers.onImageUpload,
342	    },
343	    shotWorkflow: {},
344	  }), [shotId, projectId, images, batchVideoFrames, projectAspectRatio, generationMode, handlers]);
345	
346	  return <ShotImagesEditor {...editorProps} />;
347	}
348	
349	function ShotImagesSummary({ images }: { images: PositionedImagePreview[] }) {
350	  return (
351	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
352	      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
353	        {images.slice(0, 8).map((img, idx) => (
354	          <img
355	            key={img.id || idx}
356	            src={getDisplayUrl(img.thumbUrl || img.imageUrl || img.location)}
357	            alt={`Image ${idx + 1}`}
358	            className="w-6 h-6 object-cover rounded border border-zinc-600"
359	          />
360	        ))}
361	        {images.length > 8 && (
362	          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400 ml-1">+{images.length - 8}</span>
363	        )}
364	      </div>
365	      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
366	        {images.length} image{images.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · Batch mode
367	      </span>
368	    </div>
369	  );
370	}
371	
372	// =============================================================================
373	// Final Video collapsed summary
374	// =============================================================================
375	
376	function FinalVideoSummary() {
377	  return (
378	    <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">Click to view final video output</span>
379	  );
380	}
381	
382	// =============================================================================
383	// Generation Settings collapsed summary
384	// =============================================================================
385	
386	function GenerationSettingsSummary({ settings }: { settings: VideoTravelSettings }) {
387	  const model = coerceSelectedModel(settings.selectedModel);
388	  const frames = settings.batchVideoFrames || 61;
389	  const prompt = settings.prompt || '';
390	  const truncatedPrompt = prompt.length > 60 ? prompt.slice(0, 57) + '...' : prompt;
391	
392	  return (
393	    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-400">
394	      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-300">{model}</span>
395	      <span className="text-zinc-600">·</span>
396	      <span>{frames} frames</span>
397	      {truncatedPrompt && (
398	        <>
399	          <span className="text-zinc-600">·</span>
400	          <span className="truncate max-w-[200px] italic">&ldquo;{truncatedPrompt}&rdquo;</span>
401	        </>
402	      )}
403	    </div>
404	  );
405	}
406	
407	// =============================================================================
408	// Accordion Content (combines all three sections)
409	// =============================================================================
410	
411	export interface VideoGenerationModalAccordionContentProps extends VideoGenerationModalFormContentProps {
412	  defaultTopOpen?: boolean;
413	  defaultFinalVideoOpen?: boolean;
414	  defaultBottomOpen?: boolean;
415	  shotId: string;
416	  projectId: string;
417	  positionedImages: PositionedImagePreview[];
418	  shotGenerations: GenerationRow[];
419	  effectiveAspectRatio?: string;
420	}
421	
422	export function VideoGenerationModalAccordionContent({
423	  defaultTopOpen = false,
424	  defaultFinalVideoOpen = false,
425	  defaultBottomOpen = true,
426	  shotId,
427	  projectId,
428	  positionedImages,
429	  shotGenerations,
430	  effectiveAspectRatio,
431	  ...formProps
432	}: VideoGenerationModalAccordionContentProps) {
433	  const segmentOutputs = useSegmentOutputsForShot(shotId, projectId);
434	  const hasFinalVideo = segmentOutputs.parentGenerations.some((p) => Boolean(p.location));
435	  const imageHandlers = useModalImageHandlers(shotId, projectId, shotGenerations, formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61);
436	
437	  return (
438	    <div className="space-y-4">
439	      {hasFinalVideo && (
440	        <ModalAccordionSection
441	          title="Final Video"
442	          defaultOpen={defaultFinalVideoOpen}
443	          summary={<FinalVideoSummary />}
444	        >
445	          <FinalVideoSection
446	            shotId={shotId}
447	            projectId={projectId}
448	            projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
449	            onDelete={imageHandlers.onDeleteFinalVideo}
450	          />
451	        </ModalAccordionSection>
452	      )}
453	
454	      <ModalAccordionSection
455	        title="Shot Images"
456	        defaultOpen={defaultTopOpen}
457	        summary={<ShotImagesSummary images={positionedImages} />}
458	      >
459	        <ModalShotImagesEditor
460	          shotId={shotId}
461	          projectId={projectId}
462	          images={shotGenerations}
463	          batchVideoFrames={formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61}
464	          projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
465	        />
466	      </ModalAccordionSection>
467	
468	      <ModalAccordionSection
469	        title="Generation Settings"
470	        defaultOpen={defaultBottomOpen}
471	        summary={<GenerationSettingsSummary settings={formProps.settings} />}
472	      >
473	        <VideoGenerationModalFormContent {...formProps} />
474	      </ModalAccordionSection>
475	    </div>
476	  );
477	}
478

1	/**
2	 * VideoLightbox
3	 *
4	 * Specialized lightbox for video media. Handles all video-specific functionality:
5	 * - Video trimming
6	 * - Video replace mode
7	 * - Video regenerate mode
8	 * - Video enhance mode

14	 * ImageLightbox or VideoLightbox based on media type.

17	import React from 'react';
18	import type { VideoLightboxProps, VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia } from './types';

30	import { useVideoLightboxRenderModel } from './hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel';

33	  useVideoLightboxEditing,

41	  VideoLightboxEditModel,

44	const VideoLightboxMediaContent: React.FC<VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia> = (props) => {
45	  const modeModel = useVideoLightboxMode(props);
46	  const env = useVideoLightboxEnvironment(props, modeModel);
47	  const sharedState = useVideoLightboxSharedState(props, modeModel, env);
48	  const editModel = useVideoLightboxEditing(props, modeModel, env, sharedState);
49	  const renderModel = useVideoLightboxRenderModel(props, modeModel, env, sharedState, editModel);
50	  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
51	    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
52	    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
53	    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
54	    isTabletOrLarger: sharedState.layout.isTabletOrLarger,
55	    needsFullscreenLayout: renderModel.needsFullscreenLayout,
56	    needsTasksPaneOffset: renderModel.needsTasksPaneOffset,
57	  };
58	
59	  return (
60	    <LightboxProviders stateValue={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
61	      <VideoEditProvider value={editModel.videoEditValue}>
62	        <LightboxShell
63	          onClose={props.onClose}
64	          hasCanvasOverlay={false}
65	          isRepositionMode={false}
66	          isMobile={env.isMobile}
67	          overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
68	          contentRef={env.contentRef}
69	          accessibilityTitle={renderModel.accessibilityTitle}
70	          accessibilityDescription={renderModel.accessibilityDescription}
71	        >
72	          {modeModel.isFormOnlyMode && props.segmentSlotMode ? (
73	            <SegmentSlotFormView
74	              segmentSlotMode={props.segmentSlotMode}
75	              onClose={props.onClose}
76	              onNavPrev={modeModel.handleSlotNavPrev}
77	              onNavNext={modeModel.handleSlotNavNext}
78	              hasPrevious={modeModel.hasPrevious}
79	              hasNext={modeModel.hasNext}
80	              readOnly={props.readOnly}

11	import { useSegmentSettingsForm } from '@/shared/hooks/useSegmentSettingsForm';
12	import { SegmentSettingsForm } from '@/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/SegmentSettingsForm';
13	import { extractSettingsFromParams } from '@/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/segmentSettingsMigration';
14	import { useTaskPlaceholder } from '@/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskPlaceholder';

24	function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
25	  return value && typeof value === 'object' ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
26	}
27	
28	function asNumber(value: unknown): number | undefined {
29	  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
30	}
31	
32	function asString(value: unknown): string | undefined {
33	  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
34	}
35	
36	interface SegmentRegenerateCoreProps {
37	  /** Generation params from the current video. */
38	  params: Record<string, unknown>;
39	  /** Project ID for task creation. */
40	  projectId: string | null;
41	  /** Generation ID to use as parent for the variant. */
42	  generationId: string;
43	  /** Shot ID for fetching structure video settings. */
44	  shotId?: string;
45	  /** Optional existing child generation ID (for Replace mode - creates variant instead of new child). */
46	  childGenerationId?: string;
47	  /** Optional segment index (defaults to 0 for single-segment videos). */
48	  segmentIndex?: number;
49	}
50	
51	interface SegmentRegenerateImageProps {
52	  /** Start image URL for the segment */
53	  startImageUrl?: string;
54	  /** End image URL for the segment */
55	  endImageUrl?: string;
56	  /** Start image generation ID */
57	  startImageGenerationId?: string;
58	  /** End image generation ID */
59	  endImageGenerationId?: string;
60	  /** Start image variant ID (generation_variants.id) */
61	  startImageVariantId?: string;
62	  /** End image variant ID (generation_variants.id) */
63	  endImageVariantId?: string;
64	  /** Shot generation ID for the start image (for video-to-timeline tethering) */
65	  pairShotGenerationId?: string;
66	}
67	
68	interface SegmentRegenerateFrameProps {
69	  /** Project resolution for output */
70	  projectResolution?: string;
71	  /** Callback when frame count changes - for instant timeline updates */
72	  onFrameCountChange?: (pairShotGenerationId: string, frameCount: number) => void;
73	  /** Current frame count from timeline positions (source of truth) */
74	  currentFrameCount?: number;
75	  /** Maximum frames allowed (77 with smooth continuations, 81 otherwise) */
76	  maxFrames?: number;
77	}
78	
79	interface SegmentRegenerateVariantImportProps {
80	  /** Variant params to load into the form (set externally, e.g., from VariantSelector hover) */
81	  variantParamsToLoad?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
82	  /** Callback when variant params have been loaded (to clear the trigger) */
83	  onVariantParamsLoaded?: () => void;
84	}
85	
86	interface SegmentRegenerateStructureDefaultsProps {
87	  /** Structure video type for this segment (null = no structure video coverage) */
88	  structureVideoType?: TravelGuidanceMode | null;
89	  /** Shot-level structure video defaults */
90	  structureVideoDefaults?: {
91	    mode?: TravelGuidanceMode;
92	    motionStrength: number;
93	    treatment: 'adjust' | 'clip';
94	    uni3cEndPercent: number;
95	    cannyIntensity?: number;
96	    depthContrast?: number;
97	  };
98	  structureVideoDefaultsByModel?: Partial<Record<SelectedModel, {
99	    mode?: TravelGuidanceMode;
100	    motionStrength: number;
101	    treatment: 'adjust' | 'clip';
102	    uni3cEndPercent: number;
103	    cannyIntensity?: number;
104	    depthContrast?: number;
105	  }>>;
106	  /** Structure video URL for preview */
107	  structureVideoUrl?: string;
108	  /** Frame range info for this segment's structure video usage */
109	  structureVideoFrameRange?: {
110	    segmentStart: number;
111	    segmentEnd: number;
112	    videoTotalFrames: number;
113	    videoFps: number;
114	  };
115	  /** Callback to update structure video defaults when "Set as Shot Defaults" is clicked */
116	  onUpdateStructureVideoDefaults?: (updates: {
117	    motionStrength?: number;
118	    treatment?: 'adjust' | 'clip';
119	    uni3cEndPercent?: number;
120	  }) => Promise<void>;
121	}
122	
123	interface SegmentRegenerateNavigationProps {
124	  /** Shot generation ID for the end image (for navigation) */
125	  endImageShotGenerationId?: string;
126	  /** Callback to navigate to a constituent image by shot_generation.id */
127	  onNavigateToImage?: (shotGenerationId: string) => void;
128	}
129	
130	interface SegmentRegenerateTimelineStructureProps {
131	  // Per-segment structure video management (Timeline Mode only)
132	  /** Whether in timeline mode (shows structure video upload) vs batch mode (preview only) */
133	  isTimelineMode?: boolean;
134	  /** Callback to add a structure video for this segment */
135	  onAddSegmentStructureVideo?: (video: StructureVideoConfigWithMetadata) => void;
136	  /** Callback to update this segment's structure video */
137	  onUpdateSegmentStructureVideo?: (updates: Partial<StructureVideoConfigWithMetadata>) => void;
138	  /** Callback to remove this segment's structure video */
139	  onRemoveSegmentStructureVideo?: () => void;
140	}

142	export interface SegmentRegenerateFormProps {
143	  task: SegmentRegenerateCoreProps;
144	  images: SegmentRegenerateImageProps;
145	  frame: SegmentRegenerateFrameProps;
146	  variant?: SegmentRegenerateVariantImportProps;
147	  structure?: SegmentRegenerateStructureDefaultsProps;
148	  navigation?: SegmentRegenerateNavigationProps;
149	  timeline?: SegmentRegenerateTimelineStructureProps;

152	export const SegmentRegenerateForm: React.FC<SegmentRegenerateFormProps> = ({
153	  task,
154	  images,
155	  frame,
156	  variant,
157	  structure,
158	  navigation,
159	  timeline,
160	}) => {
161	  const {
162	    params: initialParams,
163	    projectId,
164	    generationId,
165	    shotId,
166	    childGenerationId,
167	    segmentIndex = 0,
168	  } = task;
169	  const {
170	    startImageUrl,
171	    endImageUrl,
172	    startImageGenerationId,
173	    endImageGenerationId,
174	    startImageVariantId,
175	    endImageVariantId,
176	    pairShotGenerationId,
177	  } = images;
178	  const {
179	    projectResolution,
180	    onFrameCountChange,
181	    currentFrameCount,
182	    maxFrames,
183	  } = frame;
184	  const {
185	    variantParamsToLoad,
186	    onVariantParamsLoaded,
187	  } = variant ?? {};
188	  const {
189	    structureVideoType,
190	    structureVideoDefaults,
191	    structureVideoDefaultsByModel,
192	    structureVideoUrl,
193	    structureVideoFrameRange,
194	    onUpdateStructureVideoDefaults,
195	  } = structure ?? {};
196	  const {
197	    endImageShotGenerationId,
198	    onNavigateToImage,
199	  } = navigation ?? {};
200	  const {
201	    isTimelineMode,
202	    onAddSegmentStructureVideo,
203	    onUpdateSegmentStructureVideo,
204	    onRemoveSegmentStructureVideo,
205	  } = timeline ?? {};
206	
207	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
208	  const orchestratorDetails = asRecord(initialParams.orchestrator_details);
209	  const initialNumFrames = asNumber(initialParams.num_frames);
210	  const initialModelName = asString(initialParams.model_name) ?? asString(orchestratorDetails.model_name);
211	  const initialResolution = asString(initialParams.parsed_resolution_wh);
212	
213	  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
214	
215	  // Use the combined hook for form props
216	  const {
217	    formProps,
218	    getSettingsForTaskCreation,
219	    saveSettings,
220	    updateSettings,
221	    settings,
222	    enhancePromptRef,
223	  } = useSegmentSettingsForm({
224	    pairShotGenerationId,
225	    shotId,
226	    defaults: {
227	      prompt: '',
228	      negativePrompt: '',
229	      numFrames: currentFrameCount ?? initialNumFrames ?? 25,
230	      // Regeneration should replace the current video in the slot by default.
231	      // The user can uncheck this in the form to create a non-primary variant instead.
232	      makePrimaryVariant: true,
233	    },
234	    // Form display options
235	    segmentIndex,
236	    startImageUrl,
237	    endImageUrl,
238	    resolution: projectResolution ?? initialResolution,
239	    isRegeneration: true,
240	    buttonLabel: "Regenerate Video",
241	    showHeader: false,
242	    queryKeyPrefix: "lightbox-segment-presets",
243	    // Structure video
244	    structureVideoDefaults: structureVideoDefaults ?? null,
245	    structureVideoDefaultsByModel,
246	    structureVideoType,
247	    structureVideoUrl,
248	    structureVideoFrameRange,
249	    onUpdateStructureVideoDefaults,
250	    // Per-segment structure video management
251	    isTimelineMode,
252	    onAddSegmentStructureVideo,
253	    onUpdateSegmentStructureVideo,
254	    onRemoveSegmentStructureVideo,
255	    // Navigation to constituent images
256	    startImageGenerationId,
257	    endImageGenerationId,
258	    startImageShotGenerationId: pairShotGenerationId,
259	    endImageShotGenerationId,
260	    onNavigateToImage,
261	    // Frame limit
262	    maxFrames,
263	  });
264	
265	  const effectiveSelectedModel = settings.selectedModel
266	    ?? formProps.shotDefaults?.selectedModel
267	    ?? resolveSelectedModelFromModelName(initialModelName);
268	  const selectedModelName = MODEL_DEFAULTS[effectiveSelectedModel].modelName;
269	  const effectiveStructureVideoDefaults = structureVideoDefaultsByModel?.[effectiveSelectedModel]
270	    ?? structureVideoDefaults
271	    ?? null;
272	
273	  // Extract enhanced prompt from form props (enhancePromptEnabled and onEnhancePromptChange are now included in formProps)
274	  const { enhancedPrompt } = formProps;
275	
276	  // Handle frame count change - wrap to include pairShotGenerationId
277	  const handleFrameCountChange = useCallback((frameCount: number) => {
278	    if (pairShotGenerationId && onFrameCountChange) {
279	      onFrameCountChange(pairShotGenerationId, frameCount);
280	    }
281	  }, [pairShotGenerationId, onFrameCountChange]);
282	
283	  // Build structure video config from props (for task creation)
284	  const structureVideoForTask = useMemo(
285	    () => buildStructureVideoForTask(
286	      {
287	        structureVideoUrl,
288	        structureVideoType,
289	        structureVideoFrameRange,
290	        structureVideoDefaults: effectiveStructureVideoDefaults,
291	        modelName: selectedModelName,
292	      },
293	      getSettingsForTaskCreation,
294	    ),
295	    [
296	      effectiveStructureVideoDefaults,
297	      getSettingsForTaskCreation,
298	      selectedModelName,
299	      structureVideoFrameRange,
300	      structureVideoType,
301	      structureVideoUrl,
302	    ],
303	  );
304	
305	  // Effect to load variant settings when triggered from outside (e.g., VariantSelector hover button)
306	  useEffect(() => {
307	    if (!variantParamsToLoad) return;
308	
309	    const variantSettings = extractSettingsFromParams(variantParamsToLoad, {
310	      numFrames: currentFrameCount ?? settings.numFrames,
311	      makePrimaryVariant: settings.makePrimaryVariant,
312	    });
313	
314	    // Update all settings from the variant
315	    updateSettings({
316	      prompt: variantSettings.prompt,
317	      negativePrompt: variantSettings.negativePrompt,
318	      motionMode: variantSettings.motionMode,
319	      amountOfMotion: variantSettings.amountOfMotion,
320	      phaseConfig: variantSettings.phaseConfig,
321	      selectedPhasePresetId: variantSettings.selectedPhasePresetId,
322	      loras: variantSettings.loras,
323	      randomSeed: variantSettings.randomSeed,
324	      seed: variantSettings.seed,
325	      numFrames: variantSettings.numFrames,
326	    });
327	
328	    // Trigger frame count change callback if provided
329	    if (onFrameCountChange && pairShotGenerationId && variantSettings.numFrames) {
330	      onFrameCountChange(pairShotGenerationId, variantSettings.numFrames);
331	    }
332	
333	    // Notify parent that we've loaded the params (so it can clear the trigger)
334	    onVariantParamsLoaded?.();
335	  }, [
336	    variantParamsToLoad,
337	    currentFrameCount,
338	    settings.numFrames,
339	    settings.makePrimaryVariant,
340	    updateSettings,
341	    onFrameCountChange,
342	    pairShotGenerationId,
343	    onVariantParamsLoaded,
344	  ]);
345	
346	  // Handle form submission
347	  const handleSubmit = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
348	    if (!projectId) {
349	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "No project selected", variant: "destructive" });
350	      return;
351	    }
352	    if (!startImageUrl || !endImageUrl) {
353	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Missing input images", variant: "destructive" });
354	      return;
355	    }
356	
357	    submitSegmentTask({
358	      taskLabel: `Segment ${segmentIndex + 1}`,
359	      errorContext: 'SegmentRegenerateForm',
360	      getSettings: getSettingsForTaskCreation,
361	      saveSettings,
362	      shouldSaveSettings: !!pairShotGenerationId,
363	      shouldEnhance: enhancePromptRef.current,
364	      enhancedPrompt: enhancedPrompt,
365	      defaultNumFrames: currentFrameCount || 25,
366	      images: {
367	        startImageUrl,
368	        endImageUrl,
369	        startImageGenerationId,
370	        endImageGenerationId,
371	        startImageVariantId,
372	        endImageVariantId,
373	      },
374	      task: {
375	        projectId,
376	        shotId,
377	        generationId,
378	        childGenerationId,
379	        segmentIndex,
380	        pairShotGenerationId,
381	        projectResolution,
382	        modelName: selectedModelName,
383	        generationTypeMode: formProps.shotDefaults?.generationTypeMode,
384	        structureInput: structureVideoForTask,
385	        originalParams: initialParams,
386	      },
387	      run,
388	      queryClient,
389	    });
390	  }, [
391	    projectId,
392	    getSettingsForTaskCreation,
393	    saveSettings,
394	    shotId,
395	    generationId,
396	    childGenerationId,
397	    segmentIndex,
398	    startImageUrl,
399	    endImageUrl,
400	    startImageGenerationId,
401	    endImageGenerationId,
402	    startImageVariantId,
403	    endImageVariantId,
404	    pairShotGenerationId,
405	    projectResolution,
406	    selectedModelName,
407	    enhancePromptRef,
408	    run,
409	    queryClient,
410	    structureVideoForTask,
411	    currentFrameCount,
412	    enhancedPrompt,
413	  ]);
414	
415	  return (
416	    <SegmentSettingsForm
417	      {...formProps}
418	      modelName={selectedModelName}
419	      structureVideoDefaults={effectiveStructureVideoDefaults ?? undefined}
420	      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
421	      onFrameCountChange={handleFrameCountChange}
422	      edgeExtendAmount={6}
423	    />
424	  );
425	};
426

Perfect. Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive report. Let me compile the final answer:

## Summary

Based on my investigation, I found that there are **two distinct video generation surfaces** in your codebase, but they serve different purposes:

**1. Video Generation Modal (Tool Page) - `VideoGenerationModal.tsx`**
- **File path:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGenerationModal.tsx`
- **Purpose:** Batch video generation for an entire shot (all image pairs at once)
- **Uses:** `BatchSettingsForm` component + `MotionControl`
- **Context:** Opened from the travel-between-images tool page, operates in **batch mode only**

**2. Segment Settings Form (Media Lightbox) - `SegmentSettingsForm.tsx`**
- **File path:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/SegmentSettingsForm.tsx`
- **Purpose:** Per-segment video regeneration within the timeline/media-lightbox
- **Context:** Used by `SegmentRegenerateForm` and `SegmentSlotFormView` for timeline mode
- **Note:** This is per-segment, not batch

---

### **Field Comparison:**

**BatchSettingsForm** (Tool Page Modal) renders:
- Prompt (main)
- Negative Prompt
- Text Before Prompts (collapsible)
- Text After Prompts (collapsible)
- Model toggle
- Enhance Prompts (toggle)
- LTX HD Resolution (toggle, conditional)
- Smooth Continuations (toggle, conditional)
- Steps slider
- Guidance Scale slider
- Turbo Mode (toggle)
- Frames slider
- Dimensions (project/firstImage/custom)
- Motion Mode (basic/advanced)
- LoRA controls
- Phase Config (advanced mode)
- Generation Type Mode
- Random Seed (toggle)
- Amount of Motion

**SegmentSettingsForm** (Media Lightbox) renders:
- Input images (start/end) with navigation
- Frames slider
- Prompt (via PromptSection component)
- Negative Prompt (via PromptSection)
- Smooth Continuations toggle (conditional, segment index > 0)
- Model selection (via PromptSection)
- Enhanced Prompt toggle
- Advanced Settings section (steps, guidance scale, LoRA, phase config, etc.)
- Structure Video upload/management (timeline mode specific)

---

### **Root Cause of Divergence:**

1. **Different purposes:** Tool page generates batch (all pairs), media-lightbox regenerates per-segment
2. **Different shared forms:** Tool uses `BatchSettingsForm`; media-lightbox uses extracted `SegmentSettingsForm` with sub-components (`PromptSection`, `AdvancedSettingsSection`)
3. **Per-segment features:** SegmentSettingsForm includes image navigation, structure video upload, edge extend controls that don't exist in batch form
4. **Batch-specific features:** BatchSettingsForm has "Text Before/After Prompts" (not in segment form) for per-pair prompt templating

They diverged intentionally—they're solving different problems (batch vs. per-segment) and have evolved independently with no shared parent form.

127	export function VideoGenerationModalFormContent({

152	    handlePhaseConfigChange,
153	    handlePhasePresetRemove,

156	
157	  return (
158	    <div className="space-y-6 pb-4">
159	      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6">
160	        <div className="lg:w-1/2">
161	          <PanelSectionHeader title="Settings" theme="orange" />
162	          <BatchSettingsForm
163	            selectedModel={selectedModel}
164	            batchVideoPrompt={settings.prompt || ''}
165	            onBatchVideoPromptChange={(v) => updateField('prompt', v)}
166	            batchVideoFrames={settings.batchVideoFrames || 61}
167	            onBatchVideoFramesChange={(v) => updateField('batchVideoFrames', v)}
168	            batchVideoSteps={settings.batchVideoSteps || 6}
169	            onBatchVideoStepsChange={(v) => updateField('batchVideoSteps', v)}
170	            dimensionSource={settings.dimensionSource || 'firstImage'}
171	            onDimensionSourceChange={(v) => updateField('dimensionSource', v)}
172	            customWidth={settings.customWidth}
173	            onCustomWidthChange={(v) => updateField('customWidth', v)}
174	            customHeight={settings.customHeight}
175	            onCustomHeightChange={(v) => updateField('customHeight', v)}
176	            negativePrompt={settings.negativePrompt || ''}
177	            onNegativePromptChange={(v) => updateField('negativePrompt', v)}
178	            projects={projects}
179	            selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId}
180	            selectedLoras={selectedLoras}
181	            availableLoras={availableLoras}
182	            isTimelineMode={false}
183	            accelerated={accelerated}
184	            onAcceleratedChange={onAcceleratedChange}
185	            randomSeed={randomSeed}
186	            onRandomSeedChange={onRandomSeedChange}
187	            turboMode={settings.turboMode || false}
188	            onTurboModeChange={(v) => updateField('turboMode', v)}
189	            smoothContinuations={settings.smoothContinuations || false}
190	            amountOfMotion={settings.amountOfMotion || 50}
191	            onAmountOfMotionChange={(v) => updateField('amountOfMotion', v)}
192	            imageCount={imageCount}
193	            enhancePrompt={settings.enhancePrompt}
194	            onEnhancePromptChange={(v) => updateField('enhancePrompt', v)}
195	            advancedMode={(settings.motionMode || 'basic') === 'advanced'}
196	            generationTypeMode={settings.generationTypeMode || 'i2v'}
197	            phaseConfig={settings.phaseConfig || DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG}
198	            onPhaseConfigChange={handlePhaseConfigChange}
199	            selectedPhasePresetId={validPresetId}
200	            onPhasePresetSelect={handlePhasePresetSelect}
201	            onPhasePresetRemove={handlePhasePresetRemove}
202	            videoControlMode="batch"
203	            textBeforePrompts={settings.textBeforePrompts || ''}
204	            onTextBeforePromptsChange={(v) => updateField('textBeforePrompts', v)}
205	            textAfterPrompts={settings.textAfterPrompts || ''}
206	            onTextAfterPromptsChange={(v) => updateField('textAfterPrompts', v)}
207	          />
208	        </div>
209	
210	        <div className="lg:w-1/2">
211	          <PanelSectionHeader title="Motion" theme="purple" />
212	          <MotionControl

267	export function ModalAccordionSection({ title, defaultOpen = false, summary, children }: ModalAccordionSectionProps) {
268	  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);
269	
270	  return (
271	    <div className="border border-zinc-700 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
272	      <button
273	        type="button"
274	        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-800/50 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300"
275	        onClick={() => setIsOpen((prev) => !prev)}
276	      >
277	        <span>{title}</span>
278	        <ChevronDown className={`h-4 w-4 transition-transform duration-200 ${isOpen ? '' : '-rotate-90'}`} />
279	      </button>
280	      {isOpen ? (
281	        <div className="border-t border-zinc-700">{children}</div>
282	      ) : (
283	        <div className="px-4 py-3 border-t border-zinc-700/50">{summary}</div>
284	      )}
285	    </div>
286	  );
287	}

296	interface ModalShotImagesEditorProps {
297	  shotId: string;
298	  projectId: string;
299	  images: GenerationRow[];

303	
304	function ModalShotImagesEditor({ shotId, projectId, images, batchVideoFrames, projectAspectRatio }: ModalShotImagesEditorProps) {
305	  const [generationMode, setGenerationMode] = useState<'batch' | 'timeline'>('batch');
306	  const handlers = useModalImageHandlers(shotId, projectId, images, batchVideoFrames);
307	
308	  const editorProps: ShotImagesEditorProps = useMemo(() => ({
309	    displayOptions: {
310	      isModeReady: true,
311	      isMobile: false,
312	      generationMode,
313	      onGenerationModeChange: setGenerationMode as (mode: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair') => void,
314	      columns: 4 as const,
315	      skeleton: <ImageManagerSkeleton />,
316	      projectAspectRatio,
317	    },
318	    imageState: {
319	      selectedShotId: shotId,
320	      projectId,
321	      preloadedImages: images,
322	      batchVideoFrames,
323	      pendingPositions: emptyMap,
324	      unpositionedGenerationsCount: 0,
325	      fileInputKey: 0,
326	      isUploadingImage: handlers.isUploadingImage,
327	      uploadProgress: handlers.uploadProgress,
328	    },
329	    editActions: {
330	      onImageReorder: handlers.onImageReorder,
331	      onFramePositionsChange: noop,
332	      onFileDrop: handlers.onFileDrop,
333	      onGenerationDrop: handlers.onGenerationDrop,
334	      onBatchFileDrop: handlers.onBatchFileDrop,
335	      onBatchGenerationDrop: handlers.onBatchGenerationDrop,
336	      onPendingPositionApplied: noop,
337	      onImageDelete: handlers.onImageDelete,
338	      onBatchImageDelete: handlers.onBatchImageDelete,
339	      onImageDuplicate: handlers.onImageDuplicate,
340	      onOpenUnpositionedPane: noop,
341	      onImageUpload: handlers.onImageUpload,
342	    },
343	    shotWorkflow: {},
344	  }), [shotId, projectId, images, batchVideoFrames, projectAspectRatio, generationMode, handlers]);
345	
346	  return <ShotImagesEditor {...editorProps} />;
347	}
348	
349	function ShotImagesSummary({ images }: { images: PositionedImagePreview[] }) {
350	  return (
351	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
352	      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
353	        {images.slice(0, 8).map((img, idx) => (
354	          <img
355	            key={img.id || idx}
356	            src={getDisplayUrl(img.thumbUrl || img.imageUrl || img.location)}
357	            alt={`Image ${idx + 1}`}
358	            className="w-6 h-6 object-cover rounded border border-zinc-600"
359	          />
360	        ))}
361	        {images.length > 8 && (
362	          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400 ml-1">+{images.length - 8}</span>
363	        )}
364	      </div>
365	      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
366	        {images.length} image{images.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · Batch mode
367	      </span>
368	    </div>
369	  );
370	}
371	
372	// =============================================================================
373	// Final Video collapsed summary
374	// =============================================================================
375	
376	function FinalVideoSummary() {
377	  return (
378	    <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">Click to view final video output</span>
379	  );
380	}
381	
382	// =============================================================================
383	// Generation Settings collapsed summary
384	// =============================================================================
385	
386	function GenerationSettingsSummary({ settings }: { settings: VideoTravelSettings }) {
387	  const model = coerceSelectedModel(settings.selectedModel);
388	  const frames = settings.batchVideoFrames || 61;
389	  const prompt = settings.prompt || '';
390	  const truncatedPrompt = prompt.length > 60 ? prompt.slice(0, 57) + '...' : prompt;
391	
392	  return (
393	    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-400">
394	      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-300">{model}</span>
395	      <span className="text-zinc-600">·</span>
396	      <span>{frames} frames</span>
397	      {truncatedPrompt && (
398	        <>
399	          <span className="text-zinc-600">·</span>
400	          <span className="truncate max-w-[200px] italic">&ldquo;{truncatedPrompt}&rdquo;</span>
401	        </>
402	      )}
403	    </div>
404	  );
405	}

407	// =============================================================================
408	// Accordion Content (combines all three sections)
409	// =============================================================================
410	
411	export interface VideoGenerationModalAccordionContentProps extends VideoGenerationModalFormContentProps {
412	  defaultTopOpen?: boolean;
413	  defaultFinalVideoOpen?: boolean;
414	  defaultBottomOpen?: boolean;
415	  shotId: string;
416	  projectId: string;
417	  positionedImages: PositionedImagePreview[];
418	  shotGenerations: GenerationRow[];
419	  effectiveAspectRatio?: string;
420	}
421	
422	export function VideoGenerationModalAccordionContent({
423	  defaultTopOpen = false,
424	  defaultFinalVideoOpen = false,
425	  defaultBottomOpen = true,
426	  shotId,
427	  projectId,
428	  positionedImages,
429	  shotGenerations,
430	  effectiveAspectRatio,
431	  ...formProps
432	}: VideoGenerationModalAccordionContentProps) {
433	  const segmentOutputs = useSegmentOutputsForShot(shotId, projectId);
434	  const hasFinalVideo = segmentOutputs.parentGenerations.some((p) => Boolean(p.location));
435	  const imageHandlers = useModalImageHandlers(shotId, projectId, shotGenerations, formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61);
436	
437	  return (
438	    <div className="space-y-4">
439	      {hasFinalVideo && (
440	        <ModalAccordionSection
441	          title="Final Video"
442	          defaultOpen={defaultFinalVideoOpen}
443	          summary={<FinalVideoSummary />}
444	        >
445	          <FinalVideoSection
446	            shotId={shotId}
447	            projectId={projectId}
448	            projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
449	            onDelete={imageHandlers.onDeleteFinalVideo}
450	          />
451	        </ModalAccordionSection>
452	      )}
453	
454	      <ModalAccordionSection
455	        title="Shot Images"
456	        defaultOpen={defaultTopOpen}
457	        summary={<ShotImagesSummary images={positionedImages} />}
458	      >
459	        <ModalShotImagesEditor
460	          shotId={shotId}
461	          projectId={projectId}
462	          images={shotGenerations}
463	          batchVideoFrames={formProps.settings.batchVideoFrames || 61}
464	          projectAspectRatio={effectiveAspectRatio}
465	        />
466	      </ModalAccordionSection>
467	
468	      <ModalAccordionSection
469	        title="Generation Settings"
470	        defaultOpen={defaultBottomOpen}
471	        summary={<GenerationSettingsSummary settings={formProps.settings} />}
472	      >
473	        <VideoGenerationModalFormContent {...formProps} />
474	      </ModalAccordionSection>
475	    </div>
476	  );
477	}

200	            mode={{

224	            advanced={{

227	              onBlurSave: blurSaveHandler,
228	              randomSeed: generationMode.randomSeed,

232	            stateOverrides={{
233	              turboMode: motionSettings.turboMode,
234	              settingsLoading: settingsLoadingFromContext,
235	            }}
236	          />
237	        </div>
238	      </div>
239	
240	      {/* Generate CTA */}

243	          variantName={parentVariantName || ''}
244	          onVariantNameChange={parentOnVariantNameChange || (() => {})}
245	          onGenerate={() => generationHandlers.handleGenerateBatch(parentVariantName || '')}
246	          isGenerating={parentIsGeneratingVideo || generationMode.isSteerableMotionEnqueuing}
247	          justQueued={parentVideoJustQueued || generationMode.steerableMotionJustQueued}
248	          disabled={generationMode.isGenerationDisabled}

295	                  />
296	                  <Label htmlFor="stitch-after-generate" className="text-sm font-normal cursor-pointer">
297	                    Stitch generated clips
298	                  </Label>

309	              >

311	                <span>Swap to Join Segments</span>
312	              </button>
313	            ) : undefined
314	          }
315	        />
316	      </div>
317	    </>
318	  );

Perfect! Now I have a complete picture. Let me summarize the findings:

## Summary

### 1. **VideoGenerationModalFormContent Fields** (Modal/Lightbox):
Lines 162-207 in `VideoGenerationModalSections.tsx`:
- Prompt, negative prompt, text before/after prompts
- Frames, steps, amount of motion
- Dimension controls (source, width, height)
- Model selection (selectedModel only, NO `onSelectedModelChange`)
- Loras, project, image count
- Basic mode toggles: accelerated, randomSeed, turboMode
- Advanced settings: enhancePrompt, phaseConfig, phase presets
- Motion mode (advancedMode)

### 2. **BatchModeContent Fields** (Tool Page):
Lines 106-173 in `BatchModeContent.tsx`:
- **All the same fields as modal PLUS:**
  - `onSelectedModelChange` (line 108)
  - `showStepsNotification` (line 143)
  - `smoothContinuations` + `onSmoothContinuationsChange` (lines 148-149)
  - `guidanceScale` + `onGuidanceScaleChange` (lines 150-151)
  - `ltxHdResolution` + `onLtxHdResolutionChange` (lines 152-153)
  - `onBlurSave` (line 166)
  - `onClearEnhancedPrompts` (line 167)

### 3. **Root Cause: DRIFT (unintentional divergence)**

Both use `BatchSettingsForm.tsx`. The differences are **NOT intentional UX choices** but rather **incomplete prop passing**:

- **Modal omits**: `onSelectedModelChange`, `showStepsNotification`, `smoothContinuations`/`onSmoothContinuationsChange`, `guidanceScale`/`onGuidanceScaleChange`, `ltxHdResolution`/`onLtxHdResolutionChange`, `onBlurSave`, `onClearEnhancedPrompts`

The form **conditionally renders these fields** based on prop presence:
  - Model toggle: line 339 checks `if (onSelectedModelChange)`
  - Smooth continuations toggle: line 409 checks `if (canShowSmoothContinuations && onSmoothContinuationsChange)`
  - Guidance scale slider: line 478 checks `if (spec.ui.guidanceScale && guidanceScale !== undefined && onGuidanceScaleChange)`
  - LTX HD resolution: line 389 checks `if (spec.resolutionTier === 'hd' && onLtxHdResolutionChange)`
  - Clear enhanced prompts button: line 359 checks `if (onClearEnhancedPrompts)`

### 4. **Git History**:
Modal file (`VideoGenerationModalSections.tsx`): Last edited in commit `5768d1401` (snapshot)
Tool file (`BatchModeContent.tsx`): Last edited in commit `5768d1401` (snapshot)
`BatchSettingsForm.tsx`: Enhanced in `91b36279a` (fix: video editor speed/duration bugs and NumberInput migration) — much more recent than the modal call site

**The modal form hasn't been updated to pass the new conditional props that were added to BatchSettingsForm after the modal was refactored.**

**Context:** In the reigh-app, `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` exposes a `useLoraSettings()` hook that currently returns only `{ selectedLoras, availableLoras, setSelectedLoras }`. Two consumers (the `VideoGenerationModal` and the tool page's `ShotSettingsContext.loraManager`) each re-implement the same add/remove/strength/triggerWord logic against `updateField('loras', …)`. We want the provider to own these granular handlers so consumers stop duplicating them.

**Your task — modify ONLY this file:**

`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx`

In `useLoraSettings()` (around line 532), add four new handlers and include them in the returned object:

- `handleAddLora(lora: LoraModel)` — append a new `ActiveLora` to `settings.loras` (skip if the `id` already exists). See the existing impl in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/hooks/useVideoGenerationModalController.ts` lines 137-155 for reference (the logic that maps LoraModel → ActiveLora and calls `updateField('loras', [...currentLoras, newLora])`).

- `handleRemoveLora(loraId: string)` — filter `settings.loras` to drop the matching id.

- `handleLoraStrengthChange(loraId: string, strength: number)` — map over `settings.loras` updating only the matching id's `strength`.

- `handleAddTriggerWord(word: string)` — if `settings.prompt` doesn't already include `word`, append it with a comma prefix (see reference at lines 179-188 of that controller file).

**Constraints:**
1. Import types you need (`LoraModel` from `@/domains/lora/types/lora`, `ActiveLora` likewise). Check what's already imported — types are likely available via existing imports or the Handlers value. `LoraModel` may need adding.
2. Implement using the `updateField` already available via `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers()` context — the provider already has `shotSettings.updateField` in scope. Use that.
3. Return the new handlers from `useLoraSettings()` alongside the existing `{ selectedLoras, availableLoras, setSelectedLoras }`.
4. Update the `useMemo` dep array as needed to include `settings.prompt` (because `handleAddTriggerWord` reads it).
5. Do NOT modify any other file — no consumer updates, no controller changes, no modal changes. The integration step happens after your work.

1	/**
2	 * VideoTravelSettingsProvider - Centralized settings context for Video Travel tool
3	 *
4	 * This provider owns all shot-specific settings state, making it accessible to
5	 * any child component without prop drilling. Settings are persisted via useShotSettings.
6	 *
7	 * Architecture:
8	 * - Wraps useShotSettings (state + persistence)
9	 * - Wraps useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers (all update handlers)
10	 * - Exposes focused hooks for each settings domain
11	 *
12	 * Usage:
13	 * ```tsx
14	 * // In VideoTravelToolPage
15	 * <VideoTravelSettingsProvider projectId={projectId} shotId={shotId}>
16	 *   <ShotSettingsEditor />
17	 * </VideoTravelSettingsProvider>
18	 *
19	 * // In any child component
20	 * const { prompt, setPrompt } = usePromptSettings();
21	 * const { motionMode, setMotionMode } = useMotionSettings();
22	 * ```
23	 */

117	function useSeedSettingsCache(

126	    const raw = (selectedShot.settings as Record<string, unknown>)?.[TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES];
127	    if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
128	      const [REDACTED](TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES, projectId ?? undefined, shotId);
129	      // Only seed if the cache is empty — don't overwrite a more complete cascade result
130	      if (!queryClient.getQueryData(cacheKey)) {
131	        queryClient.setQueryData(cacheKey, {
132	          settings: normalizeVideoTravelSettings(raw as Record<string, unknown>),
133	          hasShotSettings: true,
134	        });
135	        console.log('[ModeDebug][CacheSeed] seeded settings cache for shot %s', shotId);
136	      }
137	    }
138	    seededRef.current = shotId;
139	  }
140	}

194	      if (!fullLora) {
195	        return [];
196	      }
197	      return [{ ...fullLora, strength: selectedLora.strength }];
198	    });
199	  }, [availableLoras, selectedLoras]);

60	): string {
61	  if (positionedImages.length === 0) {
62	    return projectAspectRatio;

64

71	  }

  "new_string": "/**\n * LoRA settings\n */\nexport function useLoraSettings() {\n  const settings = useVideoTravelSettingsData();\n  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers();\n  const availableLoras = useVideoTravelSettingsLoras();\n  return useMemo(() => {\n    const handleAddLora = (lora: LoraModel) => {\n      const newLora: ActiveLora = {\n        id: (lora['Model ID'] || '') as string,\n        name: (lora.Name || '') as string,\n        path: (lora.link || '') as string,\n        strength: 1,\n        previewImageUrl: lora['Preview Image URL'] as string | undefined,\n        trigger_word: lora['Trigger Word'] as string | undefined,\n      };\n\n      const currentLoras = settings.loras || [];\n      if (!currentLoras.some((existingLora) => existingLora.id === newLora.id)) {\n        handlers.updateField('loras', [...currentLoras, newLora]);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleRemoveLora = (loraId: string) => {\n      handlers.updateField(\n        'loras',\n        (settings.loras || []).filter((lora) => lora.id !== loraId),\n      );\n    };\n\n    const handleLoraStrengthChange = (loraId: string, strength: number) => {\n      handlers.updateField(\n        'loras',\n        (settings.loras || []).map((lora) =>\n          lora.id === loraId ? { ...lora, strength } : lora,\n        ),\n      );\n    };\n\n    const handleAddTriggerWord = (word: string) => {\n      const currentPrompt = settings.prompt || '';\n      if (!currentPrompt.includes(word)) {\n        const newPrompt = currentPrompt ? `${currentPrompt}, ${word}` : word;\n        handlers.updateField('prompt', newPrompt);\n      }\n    };\n\n    return {\n      selectedLoras: settings.loras || [],\n      availableLoras,\n      setSelectedLoras: handlers.handleSelectedLorasChange,\n      handleAddLora,\n      handleRemoveLora,\n      handleLoraStrengthChange,\n      handleAddTriggerWord,\n    };\n  }, [settings.loras, settings.prompt, availableLoras, handlers]);\n}"

45	function resolveGuidanceKind(travelGuidance?: TravelGuidance): TravelGuidanceMode | undefined {
46	  if (!travelGuidance || travelGuidance.kind === 'none') {
47	    return undefined;
48	  }
49	
50	  if (travelGuidance.kind === 'uni3c') {
51	    return 'uni3c';
52	  }
53	
54	  return travelGuidance.mode;
55	}
56	
57	function resolveEffectiveAspectRatio(
58	  projectAspectRatio: string,
59	  positionedImages: Array<{ metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null }>,
60	): string {
61	  if (positionedImages.length === 0) {
62	    return projectAspectRatio;
63	  }
64	
65	  const firstImage = positionedImages[0];
66	  const metadata = firstImage.metadata || {};
67	  const width = typeof metadata.width === 'number' ? metadata.width : null;
68	  const height = typeof metadata.height === 'number' ? metadata.height : null;
69	  if (!width || !height) {
70	    return projectAspectRatio;
71	  }
72	
73	  return findClosestAspectRatio(width / height);
74	}

129	function useVideoGenerationLoras(
130	  settings: ReturnType<typeof useShotSettings>['settings'],
131	  updateField: ReturnType<typeof useShotSettings>['updateField'],
132	  availableLoras: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof usePublicLoras>['data']> | undefined,
