```
141	        name: (lora.Name || '') as string,
142	        path: (lora.link || '') as string,
143	        strength: 1,
144	        previewImageUrl: lora['Preview Image URL'] as string | undefined,
145	        trigger_word: lora['Trigger Word'] as string | undefined,
146	      };
147	
148	      const currentLoras = settings.loras || [];
149	      if (!currentLoras.some((existingLora) => existingLora.id === newLora.id)) {
150	        updateField('loras', [...currentLoras, newLora]);
151	      }
152	      setIsLoraModalOpen(false);
153	    },
154	    [settings.loras, updateField],
155	  );
156	
157	  const handleRemoveLora = useCallback(
158	    (loraId: string) => {
159	      updateField(
160	        'loras',
161	        (settings.loras || []).filter((lora) => lora.id !== loraId),
162	      );
163	    },
164	    [settings.loras, updateField],
165	  );
166	
167	  const handleLoraStrengthChange = useCallback(
168	    (loraId: string, strength: number) => {
169	      updateField(
170	        'loras',
171	        (settings.loras || []).map((lora) =>
172	          lora.id === loraId ? { ...lora, strength } : lora,
173	        ),
174	      );
175	    },
176	    [settings.loras, updateField],
177	  );
178	
179	  const handleAddTriggerWord = useCallback(
180	    (word: string) => {
181	      const currentPrompt = settings.prompt || '';
182	      if (!currentPrompt.includes(word)) {
183	        const newPrompt = currentPrompt ? `${currentPrompt}, ${word}` : word;
184	        updateField('prompt', newPrompt);
185	      }
186	    },
187	    [settings.prompt, updateField],
188	  );
189	
190	  const selectedLorasForModal = useMemo(() => {
191	    const catalog = availableLoras || [];
192	    return selectedLoras.flatMap((selectedLora) => {
193	      const fullLora = catalog.find((model) => model['Model ID'] === selectedLora.id);
194	      if (!fullLora) {
195	        return [];
196	      }
197	      return [{ ...fullLora, strength: selectedLora.strength }];
198	    });
199	  }, [availableLoras, selectedLoras]);
200	
201	  return {
202	    isLoraModalOpen,
203	    openLoraModal: useCallback(() => setIsLoraModalOpen(true), []),
204	    closeLoraModal: useCallback(() => setIsLoraModalOpen(false), []),
205	    handleAddLora,
206	    handleRemoveLora,
207	    handleLoraStrengthChange,
208	    handleAddTriggerWord,
209	    selectedLorasForModal,
210	  };
211	}

215	export function useVideoGenerationModalController({ isOpen, onClose, shot }: {
216	  isOpen: boolean;
217	  onClose: () => void;
218	  shot: Shot;
219	}) {
220	  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
221	  const { projects } = useProjectCrudContext();
222	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
223	  const invalidateGenerations = useEnqueueGenerationsInvalidation();
224	  const { navigateToShot } = useShotNavigation();
225	  const isShotsPaneLocked = usePanesStore((state) => state.isShotsPaneLocked);
226	  const setIsShotsPaneLocked = usePanesStore((state) => state.setIsShotsPaneLocked);
227	  const { updateShotMode } = useProjectGenerationModesCache(selectedProjectId ?? '');
228	
229	  // UI state
230	  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
231	  const [justQueued, setJustQueued] = useState(false);
232	  const justQueuedTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
233	
234	  useEffect(() => {
235	    return () => {
236	      if (justQueuedTimeoutRef.current) {
237	        clearTimeout(justQueuedTimeoutRef.current);
238	      }
239	    };
240	  }, []);
241	
242	  // Data
243	  const uiSettings = useVideoUiSettings(isOpen, shot.id);
244	
245	  const { settings, status, updateField } = useShotSettings(
246	    isOpen ? shot.id : null,
247	    selectedProjectId,
248	  );
249	
250	  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
251	  const { data: shotGenerations, isLoading: generationsLoading } = useShotImages(
252	    isOpen ? shot.id : null,
253	    { disableRefetch: false },
254	  );
255	
256	  const positionedImages = useMemo(() => {
257	    if (!shotGenerations) return [];
258	    return shotGenerations
259	      .filter((gen) => !isVideoGeneration(gen) && isPositioned(gen))
260	      .sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0));
261	  }, [shotGenerations]);
262	
263	  const currentProject = projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId);
264	  const projectAspectRatio = currentProject?.aspectRatio || '16:9';
265	
266	  const effectiveAspectRatio = useMemo(
267	    () => resolveEffectiveAspectRatio(projectAspectRatio, positionedImages),
268	    [positionedImages, projectAspectRatio],
269	  );
270	
271	  const selectedLoras = useMemo(
272	    () => mapSelectedLorasToActiveLoras(settings.loras),
273	    [settings.loras],
274	  );
275	  const structureState = useMemo(
276	    () => resolveTravelStructureState(settings, {
277	      defaultEndFrame: settings.batchVideoFrames || 61,
278	      defaultVideoTreatment: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_VIDEO.treatment,
279	      defaultMotionStrength: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.motionStrength,
280	      defaultStructureType: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.structureType,
281	      defaultUni3cEndPercent: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.uni3cEndPercent,
282	    }),
283	    [settings],
284	  );
285	  const hasStructureVideo = structureState.structureVideos.length > 0;
286	  const guidanceKind = useMemo(
287	    () => resolveGuidanceKind(structureState.travelGuidance),
288	    [structureState.travelGuidance],
289	  );
290	
291	  const validPresetId = useMemo(() => {
292	    const presetId = settings.selectedPhasePresetId;
293	    if (!presetId) return undefined;
294	    return knownPresetIds.includes(presetId) ? presetId : undefined;
295	  }, [settings.selectedPhasePresetId]);
296	
297	  const isLoading =
298	    (status !== 'ready' && status !== 'saving' && status !== 'error') || generationsLoading;
299	
300	  // LoRA management (extracted — has own modal state)
301	  const loras = useVideoGenerationLoras(settings, updateField, availableLoras, selectedLoras);
302	
303	  // Navigation callbacks
304	  const handleNavigateToShot = useCallback(() => {
305	    if (isShotsPaneLocked) {
306	      setIsShotsPaneLocked(false);
307	    }
308	    onClose();
309	    navigateToShot(shot);
310	  }, [isShotsPaneLocked, setIsShotsPaneLocked, navigateToShot, onClose, shot]);
311	
312	  const handleDialogOpenChange = useCallback(
313	    (open: boolean) => {
314	      if (!open && !loras.isLoraModalOpen) {
315	        onClose();
316	      }
317	    },
318	    [loras.isLoraModalOpen, onClose],
319	  );
320	
321	  // Generate
322	  const handleGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
323	    if (!selectedProjectId || !shot.id) {
324	      toast.error('No project or shot selected.');
325	      return;
326	    }
327	
328	    if (positionedImages.length < 1) {
329	      toast.error('At least 1 positioned image is required.');
330	      return;
331	    }
332	
333	    setIsGenerating(true);
334	    try {
335	      updateField('generationMode', 'batch');
336	
337	      const userLoras = selectedLoras.map((lora) => ({
338	        path: lora.path,
339	        strength: lora.strength,
340	      }));
341	      const { phaseConfig: basicPhaseConfig } = buildBasicModePhaseConfig(
342	        settings.amountOfMotion || 50,
343	        userLoras,
344	      );
345	
346	      const motionMode = settings.motionMode || 'basic';
347	      const advancedMode = motionMode === 'advanced';
348	      const finalPhaseConfig = advancedMode
349	        ? settings.phaseConfig || DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG
350	        : basicPhaseConfig;
351	
352	      const mergedSteerableSettings = {
353	        ...DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS,
354	        ...(settings.steerableMotionSettings || {}),
355	      };
356	
357	      const result = await generateVideo({
358	        projectId: selectedProjectId,
359	        selectedShotId: shot.id,
360	        selectedShot: shot,
361	        queryClient,
362	        effectiveAspectRatio,
363	        generationMode: 'batch',
364	        promptConfig: {
365	          base_prompt: settings.prompt || '',
366	          enhance_prompt: settings.enhancePrompt,
367	          text_before_prompts: settings.textBeforePrompts,
368	          text_after_prompts: settings.textAfterPrompts,
369	          default_negative_prompt: settings.negativePrompt || mergedSteerableSettings.negative_prompt,
370	        },
371	        motionConfig: {
372	          amount_of_motion: settings.amountOfMotion || 50,
373	          motion_mode: motionMode,
374	          advanced_mode: advancedMode,
375	          phase_config: finalPhaseConfig,
376	          selected_phase_preset_id: settings.selectedPhasePresetId ?? undefined,
377	        },
378	        modelConfig: {
379	          selectedModel: coerceSelectedModel(settings.selectedModel),
380	          num_inference_steps: settings.batchVideoSteps || 6,
381	          guidance_scale: settings.guidanceScale,
382	          seed: mergedSteerableSettings.seed,
383	          random_seed: uiSettings.randomSeed,
384	          turbo_mode: settings.turboMode || false,
385	          debug: mergedSteerableSettings.debug || false,
386	          generation_type_mode: settings.generationTypeMode || 'i2v',
387	          ltxHdResolution: settings.ltxHdResolution ?? true,
388	        },
389	        travelGuidance: structureState.travelGuidance,
390	        structureGuidance: structureState.structureGuidance,
391	        structureVideos: structureState.structureVideos,
392	        batchVideoFrames: settings.batchVideoFrames || 61,
393	        selectedLoras: selectedLoras.map((lora) => ({
394	          id: lora.id,
395	          path: lora.path,
396	          strength: lora.strength,
397	          name: lora.name,
398	        })),
399	        variantNameParam: '',
400	        clearAllEnhancedPrompts,
401	      });
402	
403	      if (result.ok) {
404	        setJustQueued(true);
405	        if (justQueuedTimeoutRef.current) {
406	          clearTimeout(justQueuedTimeoutRef.current);
407	        }
408	        justQueuedTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
409	          setJustQueued(false);
410	          justQueuedTimeoutRef.current = null;
411	          onClose();
412	        }, 1000);
413	
414	        invalidateGenerations(shot.id, {
415	          reason: 'video-generation-modal-success',
416	          scope: 'all',
417	          includeProjectUnified: true,
418	          projectId: selectedProjectId ?? undefined,
419	        });
420	      } else {
421	        toast.error(result.message || 'Failed to generate video');
422	      }
423	    } catch (error) {
424	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
425	        context: 'VideoGenerationModal',
426	        toastTitle: 'Failed to generate video',
427	      });
428	    } finally {
429	      setIsGenerating(false);
430	    }
431	  }, [
432	    selectedProjectId,
433	    shot,
434	    positionedImages,
435	    updateField,
436	    selectedLoras,
437	    settings,
438	    structureState,
439	    queryClient,
440	    effectiveAspectRatio,
441	    uiSettings.randomSeed,
442	    onClose,
443	    invalidateGenerations,
444	  ]);
445	
446	  return {
447	    projects,
448	    selectedProjectId,
449	    settings,
450	    status,
451	    updateField,
452	    availableLoras,
453	    positionedImages,
454	    isLoading,
455	    isGenerating,
456	    justQueued,
457	    isDisabled: isGenerating || isLoading || positionedImages.length < 1,
458	    hasStructureVideo,
459	    guidanceKind,
460	    ...uiSettings,
461	    validPresetId,
462	    selectedLoras,
463	    ...loras,
464	    effectiveAspectRatio,
465	    shotGenerations: shotGenerations || [],
466	    handleGenerate,
467	    handleNavigateToShot,
468	    handleDialogOpenChange,
469	    updateShotMode,
470	  };
471	}
```

1	/**
2	 * VideoTravelSettingsProvider - Centralized settings context for Video Travel tool
3	 *
4	 * This provider owns all shot-specific settings state, making it accessible to
5	 * any child component without prop drilling. Settings are persisted via useShotSettings.
6	 *
7	 * Architecture:
8	 * - Wraps useShotSettings (state + persistence)
9	 * - Wraps useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers (all update handlers)
10	 * - Exposes focused hooks for each settings domain
11	 *
12	 * Usage:
13	 * ```tsx
14	 * // In VideoTravelToolPage
15	 * <VideoTravelSettingsProvider projectId={projectId} shotId={shotId}>
16	 *   <ShotSettingsEditor />
17	 * </VideoTravelSettingsProvider>
18	 *
19	 * // In any child component
20	 * const { prompt, setPrompt } = usePromptSettings();
21	 * const { motionMode, setMotionMode } = useMotionSettings();
22	 * ```
23	 */
24	
25	import React, {
26	  createContext,
27	  useCallback,
28	  useContext,
29	  useEffect,
30	  useMemo,
31	  useRef
32	} from 'react';
33	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
34	import { Shot } from '@/domains/generation/types';
35	import { useShotSettings, UseShotSettingsReturn } from '../hooks/settings/useShotSettings';
36	import {
37	  useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers as useVideoTravelSettingsHandlersCore,
38	  VideoTravelSettingsHandlers,
39	} from '../hooks/settings/useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers';
40	import {
41	  VideoTravelSettings,
42	  PhaseConfig,

44	  clampFrameCountToPolicy,
45	  coerceSelectedModel,
46	  getModelSpec,
47	  normalizeVideoTravelSettings,
48	  resolveGenerationPolicy,
49	  type SelectedModel,
50	} from '../settings';

58	
59	interface VideoTravelSettingsContextValue {
60	  // Core state
61	  settings: VideoTravelSettings;
62	  status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error';
63	  isDirty: boolean;
64	  isLoading: boolean;
65	
66	  // Shot info
67	  shotId: string | null;
68	  projectId: string | null;
69	
70	  // All handlers from useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers
71	  handlers: VideoTravelSettingsHandlers;
72	
73	  // Direct access to updateField/updateFields for custom updates
74	  updateField: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateField'];
75	  updateFields: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateFields'];
76	
77	  // Save operations
78	  save: () => Promise<void>;
79	  saveImmediate: () => Promise<void>;
80	
81	  // LoRAs (passed through from parent)
82	  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
83	}

85	interface VideoTravelSettingsStatusValue {
86	  status: VideoTravelSettingsContextValue['status'];
87	  isDirty: boolean;
88	  isLoading: boolean;
89	  shotId: string | null;
90	  projectId: string | null;
91	  save: () => Promise<void>;
92	  saveImmediate: () => Promise<void>;
93	  updateField: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateField'];
94	  updateFields: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateFields'];

97	const VideoTravelSettingsDataContext = createContext<VideoTravelSettings | null>(null);
98	const VideoTravelSettingsHandlersContext = createContext<VideoTravelSettingsHandlers | null>(null);
99	const VideoTravelSettingsStatusContext = createContext<VideoTravelSettingsStatusValue | null>(null);
100	const VideoTravelSettingsLorasContext = createContext<LoraModel[] | null>(null);
101	
102	function requireVideoTravelSettingsContext<T>(value: T | null, hookName: string): T {
103	  if (!value) {
104	    throw new Error(`${hookName} must be used within VideoTravelSettingsProvider`);
105	  }
106	  return value;
107	}

109	// =============================================================================
110	// PROVIDER COMPONENT
111	// =============================================================================
112	
113	/**
114	 * Seed the useToolSettings cache from shot data already in memory (from useListShots).
115	 * Must be called before useShotSettings so the cache is populated when useQuery runs.
116	 */
117	function useSeedSettingsCache(
118	  shotId: string | null | undefined,
119	  projectId: string | null | undefined,
120	  selectedShot: Shot | null,
121	) {
122	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
123	  const seededRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
124	
125	  if (shotId && shotId !== seededRef.current && selectedShot?.settings) {
126	    const raw = (selectedShot.settings as Record<string, unknown>)?.[TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES];
127	    if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
128	      const [REDACTED](TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES, projectId ?? undefined, shotId);
129	      // Only seed if the cache is empty — don't overwrite a more complete cascade result
130	      if (!queryClient.getQueryData(cacheKey)) {
131	        queryClient.setQueryData(cacheKey, {
132	          settings: normalizeVideoTravelSettings(raw as Record<string, unknown>),
133	          hasShotSettings: true,
134	        });
135	        console.log('[ModeDebug][CacheSeed] seeded settings cache for shot %s', shotId);
136	      }
137	    }
138	    seededRef.current = shotId;
139	  }
140	}
141	
142	interface VideoTravelSettingsProviderProps {
143	  projectId: string | null | undefined;
144	  shotId: string | null | undefined;
145	  selectedShot: Shot | null;
146	  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
147	  /** Function to optimistically update generation mode cache (from useProjectGenerationModesCache) */
148	  updateShotMode: (shotId: string, mode: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair') => void;
149	  children: React.ReactNode;
150	}
151	
152	export const VideoTravelSettingsProvider: React.FC<VideoTravelSettingsProviderProps> = ({
153	  projectId,
154	  shotId,
155	  selectedShot,
156	  availableLoras,
157	  updateShotMode,
158	  children,
159	}) => {
160	  // Seed the useToolSettings React Query cache from the shot object that's already
161	  // in memory (from useListShots). This eliminates the loading flash — the settings
162	  // fetch resolves instantly from cache instead of re-fetching the same DB row.
163	  useSeedSettingsCache(shotId, projectId, selectedShot);
164	
165	  // Core settings hook - manages state + persistence
166	  const shotSettings = useShotSettings(shotId, projectId);
167	
168	  console.log('[ModeDebug][SettingsProvider] shotId=%s status=%s generationMode=%s', shotId, shotSettings.status, shotSettings.settings?.generationMode ?? 'NOT SET');
169	
170	  // Create ref for handlers (they need ref to avoid recreation)
171	  const shotSettingsRef = useRef(shotSettings);
172	  shotSettingsRef.current = shotSettings;
173	
174	  // All handlers
175	  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlersCore({
176	    shotSettingsRef,
177	    currentShotId: shotId || null,
178	    selectedShot,
179	    updateShotMode,
180	  });
181	
182	  const setSelectedModel = useCallback((nextModel: SelectedModel) => {
183	    const currentSettings = shotSettingsRef.current.settings;
184	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
185	
186	    if (currentModel === nextModel) {
187	      return;
188	    }
189	
190	    const currentDefaults = MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel];
191	    const nextDefaults = MODEL_DEFAULTS[nextModel];
192	    const nextSpec = getModelSpec(nextModel);
193	    const currentFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
194	      currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? currentDefaults.frames,
195	      getModelSpec(currentModel),
196	      {
197	        smoothContinuations: currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false,
198	        requestedExecutionMode: currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v',
199	      },
200	    );
201	    const modelSettingsByModel = {
202	      ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
203	      [currentModel]: {
204	        batchVideoFrames: currentFrames,
205	        batchVideoSteps: currentSettings.batchVideoSteps ?? currentDefaults.steps,
206	        guidanceScale: currentSettings.guidanceScale ?? currentDefaults.guidanceScale,
207	      },
208	    };
209	    const nextSubstate = modelSettingsByModel[nextModel];
210	    const nextFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
211	      nextSubstate?.batchVideoFrames ?? nextDefaults.frames,
212	      nextSpec,
213	      {
214	        smoothContinuations: currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false,
215	        requestedExecutionMode: currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v',
216	      },
217	    );
218	
219	    shotSettingsRef.current.updateFields({
220	      selectedModel: nextModel,
221	      batchVideoFrames: nextFrames,
222	      batchVideoSteps: nextSubstate?.batchVideoSteps ?? nextDefaults.steps,
223	      guidanceScale: nextSubstate?.guidanceScale ?? nextDefaults.guidanceScale,
224	      modelSettingsByModel: {
225	        ...modelSettingsByModel,
226	        [nextModel]: {
227	          batchVideoFrames: nextFrames,
228	          batchVideoSteps: nextSubstate?.batchVideoSteps ?? nextDefaults.steps,
229	          guidanceScale: nextSubstate?.guidanceScale ?? nextDefaults.guidanceScale,
230	        },
231	      },
232	      ...(!nextSpec.ui.turboMode
233	        ? {
234	          turboMode: false,
235	          motionMode: 'basic',
236	          advancedMode: false,
237	        }
238	        : {}),
239	    });
240	  }, []);
241	
242	  useEffect(() => {
243	    const currentSettings = shotSettings.settings;
244	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
245	    const spec = getModelSpec(currentModel);
246	    const requestedExecutionMode = currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v';
247	    const nextSmoothContinuations = currentSettings.smoothContinuations
248	      && resolveGenerationPolicy(spec, {
249	        smoothContinuations: true,
250	        requestedExecutionMode,
251	      }).continuation.enabled;
252	    const normalizedFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
253	      currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].frames,
254	      spec,
255	      {
256	        smoothContinuations: nextSmoothContinuations,
257	        requestedExecutionMode,
258	      },
259	    );
260	    const currentSubstate = currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel?.[currentModel];
261	    const needsSmoothReset = (currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false) !== nextSmoothContinuations;
262	    const needsFrameReset = currentSettings.batchVideoFrames !== normalizedFrames
263	      || currentSubstate?.batchVideoFrames !== normalizedFrames;
264	
265	    if (!needsSmoothReset && !needsFrameReset) {
266	      return;
267	    }
268	
269	    shotSettings.updateFields({
270	      ...(needsSmoothReset ? { smoothContinuations: nextSmoothContinuations } : {}),
271	      ...(needsFrameReset
272	        ? {
273	          batchVideoFrames: normalizedFrames,
274	          modelSettingsByModel: {
275	            ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
276	            [currentModel]: {
277	              ...currentSubstate,
278	              batchVideoFrames: normalizedFrames,
279	            },
280	          },
281	        }
282	        : {}),
283	    });
284	  }, [
285	    shotSettings.settings.batchVideoFrames,
286	    shotSettings.settings.selectedModel,
287	    shotSettings.settings.generationTypeMode,
288	    shotSettings.settings.smoothContinuations,
289	    shotSettings.settings.modelSettingsByModel,
290	    shotSettings.updateFields,
291	  ]);
292	
293	  const setGuidanceScale = useCallback((guidanceScale: number) => {
294	    const currentSettings = shotSettingsRef.current.settings;
295	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
296	
297	    shotSettingsRef.current.updateFields({
298	      guidanceScale,
299	      modelSettingsByModel: {
300	        ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
301	        [currentModel]: {
302	          batchVideoFrames: currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].frames,
303	          batchVideoSteps: currentSettings.batchVideoSteps ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].steps,
304	          guidanceScale,
305	        },
306	      },
307	    });
308	  }, []);
309	
310	  const providerHandlers = useMemo<VideoTravelSettingsHandlers>(() => ({
311	    ...handlers,
312	    handleSelectedModelChange: setSelectedModel,
313	    handleGuidanceScaleChange: setGuidanceScale,
314	  }), [handlers, setGuidanceScale, setSelectedModel]);
315	
316	  const statusValue = useMemo<VideoTravelSettingsStatusValue>(() => ({
317	    status: shotSettings.status,
318	    isDirty: shotSettings.isDirty,
319	    isLoading: shotSettings.status === 'loading' || shotSettings.status === 'idle',
320	    shotId: shotSettings.shotId,
321	    projectId: projectId || null,
322	    updateField: shotSettings.updateField,
323	    updateFields: shotSettings.updateFields,
324	    save: shotSettings.save,
325	    saveImmediate: shotSettings.saveImmediate,
326	  }), [
327	    shotSettings.status,
328	    shotSettings.isDirty,
329	    shotSettings.shotId,
330	    shotSettings.updateField,
331	    shotSettings.updateFields,
332	    shotSettings.save,
333	    shotSettings.saveImmediate,
334	    projectId,
335	  ]);
336	
337	  return (
338	    <VideoTravelSettingsStatusContext.Provider value={statusValue}>
339	      <VideoTravelSettingsDataContext.Provider value={shotSettings.settings}>
340	        <VideoTravelSettingsHandlersContext.Provider value={providerHandlers}>
341	          <VideoTravelSettingsLorasContext.Provider value={availableLoras}>
342	            {children}
343	          </VideoTravelSettingsLorasContext.Provider>
344	        </VideoTravelSettingsHandlersContext.Provider>
345	      </VideoTravelSettingsDataContext.Provider>
346	    </VideoTravelSettingsStatusContext.Provider>
347	  );
348	};
349	
350	export function useVideoTravelSettingsStatus(): VideoTravelSettingsStatusValue {
351	  return requireVideoTravelSettingsContext(
352	    useContext(VideoTravelSettingsStatusContext),
353	    'useVideoTravelSettingsStatus',
354	  );
355	}
356	
357	export function useVideoTravelSettingsMutations() {
358	  const settings = requireVideoTravelSettingsContext(
359	    useContext(VideoTravelSettingsDataContext),
360	    'useVideoTravelSettingsMutations',
361	  );
362	  const status = useVideoTravelSettingsStatus();
363	
364	  return {
365	    settings,
366	    status: status.status,
367	    shotId: status.shotId,
368	    updateField: status.updateField,
369	    updateFields: status.updateFields,
370	  };
371	}

373	export function useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers() {
374	  return requireVideoTravelSettingsContext(
375	    useContext(VideoTravelSettingsHandlersContext),

389	    useContext(VideoTravelSettingsLorasContext),
390	    'useVideoTravelSettingsLoras',
391	  );
392	}

398	  const {

400	    isDirty,
401	    isLoading,
402	    shotId,
403	    projectId,
404	    save,
405	    saveImmediate,
406	    updateField,
407	    updateFields,
408	  } = useVideoTravelSettingsStatus();
409	  const availableLoras = useVideoTravelSettingsLoras();
410	
411	  return {
412	    settings,
413	    status,
414	    isDirty,
415	    isLoading,

417	    projectId,

431	/**
432	 * Prompt-related settings
433	 */
434	export function usePromptSettings() {
435	  const settings = useVideoTravelSettingsData();
436	  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers();
437	  return useMemo(() => ({
438	    prompt: settings.prompt || '',
439	    negativePrompt: settings.negativePrompt || '',
440	    textBeforePrompts: settings.textBeforePrompts || '',
441	    textAfterPrompts: settings.textAfterPrompts || '',
442	    enhancePrompt: settings.enhancePrompt,
443	    setPrompt: handlers.handleBatchVideoPromptChange,
444	    setNegativePrompt: handlers.handleNegativePromptChange,
445	    setTextBeforePrompts: handlers.handleTextBeforePromptsChange,
446	    setTextAfterPrompts: handlers.handleTextAfterPromptsChange,
447	    setEnhancePrompt: handlers.handleEnhancePromptChange,
448	  }), [settings.prompt, settings.negativePrompt, settings.textBeforePrompts, settings.textAfterPrompts, settings.enhancePrompt, handlers]);
449	}
450	
451	/**
452	 * Motion-related settings
453	 */
454	export function useMotionSettings() {

461	    turboMode: settings.turboMode ?? false,

463	    setAmountOfMotion: handlers.handleAmountOfMotionChange,
464	    setMotionMode: handlers.handleMotionModeChange,
465	    setTurboMode: handlers.handleTurboModeChange,
466	    setSmoothContinuations: handlers.handleSmoothContinuationsChange,
467	    availableLoras,
468	  }), [settings.amountOfMotion, settings.motionMode, settings.turboMode, settings.smoothContinuations, handlers, availableLoras]);
469	}

471	/**
472	 * Frame/duration settings

495	  }), [settings.selectedModel, settings.guidanceScale, settings.ltxHdResolution, handlers]);

504	  return useMemo(() => ({
505	    phaseConfig: settings.phaseConfig,
506	    selectedPhasePresetId: settings.selectedPhasePresetId,
507	    generationTypeMode: settings.generationTypeMode || 'i2v',
508	    advancedMode: settings.advancedMode ?? false,
509	    setPhaseConfig: handlers.handlePhaseConfigChange,
510	    selectPreset: handlers.handlePhasePresetSelect,
511	    removePreset: handlers.handlePhasePresetRemove,
512	    setGenerationTypeMode: handlers.handleGenerationTypeModeChange,
513	    restoreDefaults: handlers.handleRestoreDefaults,
514	  }), [settings.phaseConfig, settings.selectedPhasePresetId, settings.generationTypeMode, settings.advancedMode, handlers]);
515	}

517	/**
518	 * Steerable motion settings (seed, model, etc.)
519	 */
520	export function useSteerableMotionSettings() {
521	  const settings = useVideoTravelSettingsData();
522	  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers();
523	  return useMemo(() => ({
524	    steerableMotionSettings: settings.steerableMotionSettings,
525	    setSteerableMotionSettings: handlers.handleSteerableMotionSettingsChange,
526	  }), [settings.steerableMotionSettings, handlers]);
527	}
528	
529	/**
530	 * LoRA settings
531	 */
532	export function useLoraSettings() {
533	  const settings = useVideoTravelSettingsData();
534	  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers();
535	  const availableLoras = useVideoTravelSettingsLoras();
536	  return useMemo(() => {
537	    const handleAddLora = (lora: LoraModel) => {
538	      const newLora: ActiveLora = {
539	        id: (lora['Model ID'] || '') as string,
540	        name: (lora.Name || '') as string,
541	        path: (lora.link || '') as string,
542	        strength: 1,
543	        previewImageUrl: lora['Preview Image URL'] as string | undefined,
544	        trigger_word: lora['Trigger Word'] as string | undefined,
545	      };
546	
547	      const currentLoras = settings.loras || [];
548	      if (!currentLoras.some((existingLora) => existingLora.id === newLora.id)) {
549	        handlers.updateField('loras', [...currentLoras, newLora]);
550	      }
551	    };
552	
553	    const handleRemoveLora = (loraId: string) => {
554	      handlers.updateField(
555	        'loras',
556	        (settings.loras || []).filter((lora) => lora.id !== loraId),
557	      );
558	    };
559	
560	    const handleLoraStrengthChange = (loraId: string, strength: number) => {
561	      handlers.updateField(
562	        'loras',
563	        (settings.loras || []).map((lora) =>
564	          lora.id === loraId ? { ...lora, strength } : lora,
565	        ),
566	      );
567	    };
568	
569	    const handleAddTriggerWord = (word: string) => {
570	      const currentPrompt = settings.prompt || '';
571	      if (!currentPrompt.includes(word)) {
572	        const newPrompt = currentPrompt ? `${currentPrompt}, ${word}` : word;
573	        handlers.updateField('prompt', newPrompt);
574	      }
575	    };
576	
577	    return {
578	      selectedLoras: settings.loras || [],
579	      availableLoras,
580	      setSelectedLoras: handlers.handleSelectedLorasChange,
581	      handleAddLora,
582	      handleRemoveLora,
583	      handleLoraStrengthChange,
584	      handleAddTriggerWord,
585	    };
586	  }, [settings.loras, settings.prompt, availableLoras, handlers]);
587	}
588	
589	/**

592	export function useGenerationModeSettings() {

596	  console.log('[ModeDebug][GenModeSettings] shotId=%s raw generationMode=%s resolved=%s', shotId, settings.generationMode, settings.generationMode || 'timeline');
597	  return useMemo(() => ({
598	    generationMode: settings.generationMode || 'timeline',
599	    videoControlMode: settings.videoControlMode || 'batch',
600	    setGenerationMode: handlers.handleGenerationModeChange,
601	    setVideoControlMode: handlers.handleVideoControlModeChange,
602	  }), [settings.generationMode, settings.videoControlMode, handlers]);
603	}

605	/**
606	 * Save operations
607	 */

615	    isDirty,
616	    isSaving: status === 'saving',
617	  }), [save, saveImmediate, handlers, isDirty, status]);
618	}

File contents:

```ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useToolSettings } from '@/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings';
import { SETTINGS_IDS } from '@/shared/lib/settingsIds';

/** Per-shot UI toggle persistence (accelerated mode, random seed). Own external hook dep. */
export function useVideoUiSettings(isOpen: boolean, shotId: string) {
  const { settings: shotUISettings, update: updateShotUISettings } = useToolSettings<{
    acceleratedMode?: boolean;
    randomSeed?: boolean;
  }>(SETTINGS_IDS.TRAVEL_UI_STATE, {
    shotId: isOpen ? shotId : undefined,
    enabled: isOpen && Boolean(shotId),
  });

  const accelerated = shotUISettings?.acceleratedMode ?? false;
  const randomSeed = shotUISettings?.randomSeed ?? false;

  const setAccelerated = useCallback(
    (value: boolean) => { updateShotUISettings('shot', { acceleratedMode: value }); },
    [updateShotUISettings],
  );

  const setRandomSeed = useCallback(
    (value: boolean) => { updateShotUISettings('shot', { randomSeed: value }); },
    [updateShotUISettings],
  );

  return { accelerated, randomSeed, setAccelerated, setRandomSeed };
}
```

```
import type { PersistedGenerationRow } from './generationRow';

/**
 * Derived display fields (read-only, produced by mappers/selectors from persisted data).
 * Ownership: mapper/query layer.
 */
interface GenerationDerivedFields {
  imageUrl?: string;
  thumbUrl?: string;
  contentType?: string;
  derivedCount?: number;
  hasUnviewedVariants?: boolean;
  unviewedVariantCount?: number;
}

/** Domain-facing generation contract = persisted row + derived view fields. */
export type GenerationRow = PersistedGenerationRow & GenerationDerivedFields;
```

**Report**

Created `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/hooks/useBatchVideoGeneration.ts`.

**Signature**
```ts
useBatchVideoGeneration({
  shot: Shot,
  projectId: string | null | undefined,
  onClose: () => void,
  randomSeed: boolean,
  positionedImages: Array<{ metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null }>,
  effectiveAspectRatio: string,
  selectedLoras: ActiveLora[],
  structureState: ReturnType<typeof resolveTravelStructureState>,
}) => { handleGenerate, isGenerating, justQueued, isDisabled }
```

**Dependency surface**
- `useQueryClient` from `@tanstack/react-query`
- `useEnqueueGenerationsInvalidation` from `@/shared/hooks/invalidation/useGenerationInvalidation`
- `useVideoTravelSettingsMutations` from the provider (gives `settings` + `updateField`)
- `toast`, `normalizeAndPresentError`, `generateVideo`, `buildBasicModePhaseConfig`, `DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS`, `DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG`, `coerceSelectedModel` — all per spec.
- React: `useState`, `useRef`, `useEffect`, `useCallback`.

**Design calls**
- Per the task instructions' later refinement, shot-data derivations (`positionedImages`, `effectiveAspectRatio`, `selectedLoras`, `structureState`) are passed in as parameters rather than re-derived. This keeps the hook purely orchestration-focused; the controller keeps ownership of `useShotImages`, aspect-ratio resolution, and the `settings.loras` → `ActiveLora[]` mapping.
- Settings for the generate payload are sourced from `useVideoTravelSettingsMutations()` (provider `settings` + `updateField`) — not from the focused hooks — since `generateVideo` needs many fields at once and that matched the "easiest path" note in the task.
- The task listed `shotGenerations` as a parameter, but no branch of the original `handleGenerate` uses it. Omitted to keep the surface minimal; can add later if a consumer needs it.
- `clearAllEnhancedPrompts` is kept as a local no-op stub, per spec.
- Behavior is preserved verbatim: same toast strings, same `invalidateGenerations` scope (`'all'` + `includeProjectUnified: true`, reason `video-generation-modal-success`), same 1000 ms `justQueued` window, same cleanup ref.

The existing `useVideoGenerationModalController.ts` was not modified, per the constraint.

> DEVELOPER

Working directory: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

**Context:** `BatchSettingsForm` in the reigh-app renders video-generation controls. Several fields are gated on both a model-spec capability AND the presence of the corresponding handler prop. If a consumer forgets to pass a handler, the field silently disappears — no error, no warning. This recently caused a real bug: a modal consumer was missing `onSelectedModelChange`, `onSmoothContinuationsChange`, `onGuidanceScaleChange`, and `onLtxHdResolutionChange` and dropped four fields silently.

**Your task — modify ONLY this file:**

`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/BatchSettingsForm.tsx`

Add a dev-only runtime warning that `console.warn`s when the model spec says a field should render but its required handler is missing. This catches the same bug class at dev time.

**Specifics:**

1. Read `BatchSettingsForm.tsx` top-to-bottom first, then search for the conditional render checks that gate on `spec.ui.*` / `canShow*` + handler presence. From prior analysis, the relevant checks include:
   - Smooth Continuations field (around line 409): gated on `canShowSmoothContinuations && onSmoothContinuationsChange`
   - Guidance Scale slider (around line 478): gated on `spec.ui.guidanceScale && guidanceScale !== undefined && onGuidanceScaleChange`
   - LTX HD Resolution toggle (around line 389): gated on `spec.resolutionTier === 'hd' && onLtxHdResolutionChange`
   - Model toggle (around line 339): gated on `onSelectedModelChange`
   
   Verify these line numbers and find any others I missed (e.g. anything else referencing `spec.ui.*` alongside an optional `on*Change` prop).

2. Add a single `useEffect` near the top of the component body (after `const spec = getModelSpec(selectedModel)` / similar derivations) that emits warnings like:
   
   ```
   [BatchSettingsForm] Model "<selectedModel>" supports guidanceScale but no onGuidanceScaleChange handler was passed — the guidance scale slider will not render. Check the callsite.
   ```
   
   One warning per missing handler. Trigger only when the spec says the field applies.

3. The warning must be **dev-only**. Use `import.meta.env.DEV` (Vite) as the guard — check existing code in the file or other files in the repo for the established pattern. Do NOT use `process.env.NODE_ENV` unless the codebase already does.

4. Do NOT change render behavior. The useEffect is purely diagnostic. Fields that are missing handlers should still not render — that's the existing contract; we're just making the drop loud in development.

5. Do NOT add the warning for the model toggle (`onSelectedModelChange`) — that handler being optional is legitimate for read-only preview surfaces. Only warn when the spec indicates the field should be user-editable for the current model.

6. Deps: include `selectedModel` (so warnings re-emit when the user switches models) and the handler props themselves in the useEffect dep array.

**Verification:**

1. `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app` — must pass with no new errors.
2. If there's a test file for BatchSettingsForm, run it. Search: `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/BatchSettingsForm.test.*`. If it exists, do NOT break it. Don't add new test coverage unless the existing file is easy to extend — the user wants minimal churn.

**Report:** list of warnings you added (field name + trigger condition), any additional gated fields you discovered beyond the four above, and confirmation of passing typecheck/tests. Under 250 words.

```
442	                      }`}

444	                      Distilled

447	                      type="button"

450	                        spec.id === 'ltx-2.3'
451	                          ? 'bg-muted text-foreground'

453	                      }`}
454	                    >
455	                      Full
456	                    </button>

458	                )}

462	                  </Label>

465	                    min={stepMin}

470	                    disabled={readOnly}

472	                  />
473	                </div>
474	              </div>
475	            )}
476	
477	            {/* Guidance Scale */}
478	            {spec.ui.guidanceScale && guidanceScale !== undefined && onGuidanceScaleChange && (
479	              <div className="relative">
480	                <Label htmlFor="guidanceScale" className="text-sm font-light block mb-1">
481	                  Guidance scale: {guidanceScale.toFixed(1)}
482	                </Label>
483	                <Slider
484	                  id="guidanceScale"
485	                  min={1}
486	                  max={10}
487	                  step={0.1}
488	                  value={guidanceScale}
489	                  onValueChange={(value) => {
490	                    const v = Array.isArray(value) ? (value[0] ?? guidanceScale) : value;
491	                    onGuidanceScaleChange(Number(Number(v).toFixed(1)));
492	                  }}
493	                  disabled={readOnly}
494	                  className={readOnly ? 'opacity-50' : ''}
495	                />
496	              </div>
497	            )}
498	            
499	            {/* Turbo Mode Toggle - DISABLED - keeping code for potential future use
500	            {isCloudGenerationEnabled && !isTurboModeDisabled && (
501	              <div className="flex items-center gap-x-2 p-3 bg-muted/30 rounded-lg border">
502	                <Switch
503	                  id="turbo-mode"
504	                  checked={turboMode}
505	                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
506	                    onTurboModeChange(checked);
507	                    // Auto-set frames to 81 when turbo mode is enabled
508	                    if (checked && batchVideoFrames !== 81) {
509	                      onBatchVideoFramesChange(81);
510	                    }
511	                  }}
512	                />
513	                <div className="flex-1">
514	                  <Label htmlFor="turbo-mode" className="font-medium">
515	                    Turbo Mode
516	                  </Label>
517	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
518	                    Using fast WAN 2.2 model for quick results ({framesToSeconds(81)})
519	                  </p>
520	                </div>
521	              </div>
522	            )}
523	            */}
524	            
525	            {/* Frames per pair - hidden in Timeline mode since users set duration individually */}
526	            {!isTimelineMode && (
527	              <div className="relative">
528	                <Label htmlFor="batchVideoFrames" className="text-sm font-light block mb-1">
529	                  {imageCount === 1 ? 'Duration to generate' : 'Duration per pair'}: {framesToSeconds(resolvedFrameCount, modelDefaults.fps)} ({resolvedFrameCount} frames)
530	                </Label>
531	                <ResponsiveInfoTip
532	                  isMobile={isMobile}

535	                      Determines the duration of the video segment{imageCount === 1 ? '' : ' for each image'}.
536	                      <br />
537	                      More frames result in a longer segment.
538	                    </p>
539	                  )}
540	                />
541	                <Slider
542	                  id="batchVideoFrames"
543	                  min={9}
544	                  max={maxFrames}
545	                  step={frameStep}
546	                  value={resolvedFrameCount}
547	                  onValueChange={(value) => onBatchVideoFramesChange(clampFrameCountToPolicy(value, spec, generationIntent))}
548	                  disabled={turboMode}
549	                  className={turboMode ? 'opacity-50' : ''}
550	                />
551	              </div>
552	            )}
553	
554	
555	        </div>
556	    );
557	};
```

> AGENT

Done.

**Warnings added** (all guarded by `import.meta.env.DEV`, in a single `useEffect`):

1. **Smooth Continuations / Continue toggle** — fires when `canShowSmoothContinuations` is true but `onSmoothContinuationsChange` is missing. (Gate at line 409.)
2. **Guidance Scale slider** — fires when `spec.ui.guidanceScale` is true but `onGuidanceScaleChange` is missing. (Gate at line 478.)
3. **LTX HD Resolution toggle** — fires when `spec.resolutionTier === 'hd'` but `onLtxHdResolutionChange` is missing. (Gate at line 389.)

**Other gated fields discovered, and why they're excluded:**
- **Model toggle** (line 339): gated on `onSelectedModelChange` only; per spec, the optional handler is legitimate for read-only preview surfaces — explicitly excluded per instructions.
- **Distilled/Full LTX sub-toggle** (line 433): gated on `onSelectedModelChange && spec.modelFamily === 'ltx'` — same rationale; read-only-friendly optional handler.
- **Inference Steps block** (line 431): gated only on `spec.ui.inferenceSteps`; no optional handler, so no silent-drop risk.

**Deps** on the useEffect: `selectedModel`, `canShowSmoothContinuations`, `spec.ui.guidanceScale`, `spec.resolutionTier`, and the three handler props — so warnings re-emit when the user switches models.

**Render behavior unchanged** — effect is purely diagnostic; existing conditional gates still drop fields when a handler is absent.

- `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app`: passes with zero output, exit 0.
- No test file exists at `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/BatchSettingsForm.test.*` (Glob returned nothing); nothing to break, no new coverage added per minimal-churn direction.
